# Bug burn-down

Started 2026-10-16 on `claude/bug-burn-down`, from `main`.

## Method

- Baseline: full test suite (`pnpm test`), typecheck (`pnpm check`) and lint
  (`pnpm lint`) on Node 24, the version `.node-version` names. (Node 22 fails two
  Web Locks tests in `src/lib/web/sync-store.test.ts` that pass on 24; that is the
  environment, not a bug.)
- Audit: a read-only pass over the highest-risk areas, each finding traced to a
  concrete input before it was kept: the sync Worker, the Tauri backend's path
  judges, markdown rendering and the trust rule, sync-core / rooms / chats /
  online, the workspace store, bases and the editor's commands, and the machine
  service, the clipper, the CLI and CI.
- Each bug: reproduced with a failing regression test, root cause named, fix, then
  the package's tests, typecheck, prettier and eslint re-run. One commit per bug.

Severity: **Critical** - cross-user exposure, code execution or broad silent
data loss. **High** - data loss or a security hole on a specific path.
**Medium** - wrong behaviour in normal use. **Low** - cosmetic or a rare edge.

## Summary

| Severity | Found | Fixed | Open |
| --- | --: | --: | --: |
| Critical | 1 | 1 | 0 |
| High | 14 | 14 | 0 |
| Medium | 35 | 35 | 0 |
| Low | 16 | 11 | 5 |

Open rows are being fixed; see the status column.

| B1 | Critical | `PUT /v1/blobs/:hash` stored any bytes under any hash, replacing the one object every account with that hash is served: stored XSS on published sites via `/i/<hash>.js`, defaced pictures | Fixed `caf8bdfb` | The route took the hash in the path on trust and always wrote `blobs/<hash>` | Bytes are hashed and must be the name; the one exception (a drawn diagram, named by fence and unprinted space id) is never written over an object already there; a verified upload repairs an object that is not its name | `blobs.test.ts` "bytes under a hash that is not theirs" (4 tests, 2 failed before); sync suite 2052 passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B2 | High | A failed multipart upload could leave junk under another account's file | Fixed `78c9095c` | Parts were put together at `blobs/<hash>` itself; whether a copy existed was asked only at the start | Parts go to `uploads/<hash>/<uuid>`, are measured and hashed, then copied into place only if no copy is there | `sync2-files.test.ts` "never over a copy finished meanwhile" (failed before); sync suite passed |
| B3 | High | Running `release.yml` by hand on any branch replaced the signed `edge` release, which edge installs update to | Fixed `f7158d4b` | `build-version.sh` makes every non-tag ref `edge`, and `publish` had no ref guard | `publish` runs only for `main` or a `v*` tag, as `publish-mobile.yml` does | `packaging.test.ts` "publishes only from main or a tag" (failed before), "and no other job writes" |
| B4 | Medium | Knowing a hash was enough to read a non-image file | Fixed `a2425ab0` | Starting a multipart upload for a hash someone held wrote a `blobs` row with no bytes; an SVG under a held diagram name did the same | The upload is sent in full and the row follows the check; an unverified SVG under a held name is refused. (Chat posts were already checked by `holds`, which is sound once a row means bytes.) | `sync2-files.test.ts` "sent in full even when somebody else already keeps them", `blobs.test.ts` diagram row assertion (both failed before) |
| B5 | Medium | A v1 save renaming a note onto a taken path, or reviving a deleted note over a new one, answered 500 | Fixed `6969078c` | No occupancy check before the update; the unique index threw | Same 409 and note as a create, from a read before the write and from a caught race | `sync.test.ts` "a save that moves a note onto another one is told it is there", "…brings a deleted note back over a new one" (500 before) |
| B6 | Medium | A watcher who may not type restarted the owner's ended shell by opening or reconnecting | Fixed `8c120fa6` | `hello` called `ensureOpen` for every viewer and nibd restarts a dead session on `open` | `ensureOpen` skips an ended session for a viewer who may not type; `reopen` prefers a typist | `machines.test.ts` "a watcher arriving on an ended session does not start it again" (failed before); 82 passed |
| B7 | Medium | A 52-90 ms long task at launch after `rooms may start`; `smoke` red on every PR | Fixed `53cd7d68` | It was the doors stage: `warmDoors()` started ~36 dynamic imports in one task, and Vite's preload helper scanned every `<link>` and appended a modulepreload per new dependency for each | `knock()` starts each door after `breathe()`, so each import's preload work is its own task; failures still reject the whole | `long-tasks.py` same machine, interleaved: without the fix 4/4 failed (73-138 ms at the doors mark), with it 4/4 passed; profiles before 130-163 ms at doors, after none. A separate 60-120 ms left-panel flush under heavy load predates this and was not seen on CI |

| B56 | Low | An agent or automation edit can report done when the note changed in the same milliseconds and nothing was written | Open, accepted | `writeClosed` now refuses an overlapping edit; the caller does not hear of it | - (before this branch the same race wrote over the note) | - |

| B62 | Medium | On a desktop, a folder deleted on another device was never removed: sync forgot the notes in it and sent them up again (re-audit of B61; older than this branch) | Fixed `64b147da4` | The crate stamps no folder, so `exists(folder)` was false and `forgetUnder` dropped the notes inside without removing them | `onDisk` on the desktop also asks the tree, which refuses a root that is not a folder | `on-disk.test.ts` (failed before) |

| B64 | Medium | A folder moved into a new folder that took its old name was asked to move inside itself, and sync threw on every pass (re-audit of B63; older than this branch) | Fixed `45fff1553` | The new folder has no place on disk yet, so nothing marked the target as inside the entry's own path | An entry wanted inside where it is steps aside first and moves on next round | `sync2/places.test.ts` with a disk that refuses a move into itself (failed before); 3000 random trees in a scratch fuzz all reach their wanted layout |
| B65 | Medium | In the browser, an import read the whole listing once for every file it brought (complete final re-audit; regression from B61) | Fixed `b21d6d6c6` | `onDisk` asked a missing name by listing a tree, which reads every row in the store | `path_exists`, a key lookup on the listing (`stats.holds`), answers for notes, assets and folders in one call | `web/store.test.ts`, `web/commands.test.ts`, `on-disk.test.ts` |

Not fixed, judged insignificant or out of scope: GFM's escaped backslash before a
pipe in tables (text written back unchanged); Todoist `date before: Oct 6, 2026`
(the comma splits the filter; unclear Todoist accepts it); NFC/NFD names treated as
one file on ext4 (not reproduced).

## Round 2

Started 2026-10-23 on `claude/inspiring-feynman-tip6ma`, from `main` at `832b602`
(v0.18.0). It continues from round 1: the audit covers what changed since round 1
ended (`7a53572..832b602`, 383 commits), and round 1's open Low rows are looked at
again. Each fixed bug has its own branch (`claude/inspiring-feynman-tip6ma-bNN`) and
pull request off `main`; this log is on the round's own branch.

Same method and severity scale as round 1.

| # | Severity | Bug | Status | Root cause | Fix | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| B67 | Medium | On a board with swimlanes, a card dragged within a lane could snap back, most often in the lane of no value | Fixed, [#681](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/681) | The board writes a lane's order into its column's; `answer()` sorted each lane again by the order under the lane's own name, which is a column's (the no-value lane read the No-value column's stale order) | A sub-group keeps the order its group gave its members | `answer.test.ts` "a lane keeps its column's order…" (failed before); bases 803 passed, desktop views 145 passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B68 | Low | Two tasks with the same words in one note cannot be ordered apart on a board | Open, accepted | `orderId` names a task by its words' hash, which the two share | - (only the relative order of two identical lines) | Reproduced by a scratch test |
| B69 | High | Any account could replace a drawn diagram on a published page, and the owner could never draw it again | Fixed, [#683](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/683) | A diagram's name is the sha256 of a string a reader of the space can write; sent as a file, those bytes were "their own name", so the repair branch put them over the drawing | Bytes that spell a diagram's name are refused on the v1, v2 and multipart uploads | `blobs.test.ts` "that spell out a diagram's name…" (failed before); sync 2164 passed, markdown 1208 passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B70 | High | When both devices deleted the same task and each added a different one, the merge was called clean and one added task lost its `- [ ] `, leaving every task view and no version kept (regression from #530) | Fixed, [#685](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/685) | `putTwice` counted the markers `-`, `[`, `]` as words moved twice, and `withoutWord` took them out of the older side's line | A word with no letter or digit is never counted as moved | `diverge.test.ts` "a task each side added, after one both deleted, keeps its box", both time orders, with and without the CRDT text (failed before); sync-core 205 passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B71 | Medium | A task nobody touched was dropped (kept only as a version) when one side added a line at the top and the other at the bottom, and both deleted the same one (older than round 2) | Fixed, [#694](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/694), reworked with B110 | The semantic character diff read a line added above a line deleted as every line between rewritten, so the two sides seemed to rewrite the deleted line two ways | The semantic diff compares whole lines first, then each run of changed lines by character | `diverge.test.ts` "…either side of one both deleted, leaves the rest alone" (failed before); sync-core 205 passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean; `sim:long` sampled; follow-up 2195e83f3 (seed 7808 of the long walk): a newer side that repeats something is no longer kept to dodge a line nobody wrote, the merge is held instead, `diverge.test.ts`, 10,000 seeds pass |
| B72 | Medium | A merge could write a word neither side wrote (`wa task`) when two new tasks began with the same word (older than round 2) | Fixed, [#697](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/697) | `repeats` counted the `- [ ] ` punctuation into its runs, read the two tasks as one passage said twice, and fell back to the CRDT's woven text | Runs count only letters and digits | `diverge.test.ts` "two new tasks that start with the same word are not one passage said twice" (failed before); sync-core 205 passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B73 | Medium | Pages whose names hold letters outside ASCII (`Über mich`) answered on new addresses after 0.18.0, and their old ones were 404s | Fixed, [#686](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/686) | `slugFor`/`cleanSlug` began keeping such letters, and nothing recorded the old addresses | The old ASCII form is worked out again and redirected (301) before a 404 | `blog.test.ts` "the address a page had before its letters were kept…" (failed before); sync 2164 passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B74 | Medium | A scheduled chat post that threw while being placed woke the chat again at once, without end, and held back every post due after it | Fixed, [#691](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/691) | `placeDue` left its loop on the first throw, and `plan()` set the alarm to the past `send_at` | Each row is placed on its own; one that throws is tried again after 30 s, doubling to an hour | `chats.test.ts` "one that keeps throwing is tried again later…" (failed before); sync 2164 passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B75 | Medium | "Add task" from Shortcuts on a Mac added nothing on a new install, or wrote into a folder the app does not show | Fixed, [#689](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/689) | `siri::spaces_dir` kept the rule from before #376 (`Documents/Nib` only) instead of the folder the app chose | The same rule as `chosen_spaces`: the kept folder, else `Documents/Nib` where it is, else the app's `Spaces` | `siri.rs` `the_spaces_folder_is_the_one_the_app_chose` (macOS CI; test pushed alone first) |
| B76 | Medium | A page note copied into a folder holding `X.pages.pdf` (as macOS Pages names its PDFs) took that PDF for its paper, and deleting the copy trashed it | Fixed, [#693](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/693) | The #538 guard was per command and `copy_path` lacked it; `freeName` knew nothing of papers | `freeName` counts a page note's paper name as taken, and `copied` refuses one | `paper-names.store.test.ts` (failed before), `notes.rs` `a_page_note_copied_never_takes_a_pdf_already_there` (CI); desktop 948 passed in the touched dirs, svelte-check 0 errors |
| B77 | Medium | A `.zip` attached in an import (a folder or an export) was unpacked into the import's top level and the zip itself left out, so its link was dead | Fixed, [#687](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/687) | `sourcesFrom` and `expand` opened every `.zip` by its name | A nested zip is opened only where everything beside it is a zip too (Notion's parts) | `sources.test.ts` (failed before); import 521 passed; svelte-check 0 errors; eslint, prettier clean |
| B78 | Medium | One `.enex` or Tomboy `.note` among markdown files made the whole import that format, and every other file was dropped silently | Fixed, [#690](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/690) | `detect` chose the format on the first such file; those readers read only their own | Those formats only where no notes sit beside them | `read.test.ts` "a folder of notes with an Evernote file or a Tomboy note kept in it…" (failed before); import 519 passed; svelte-check 0 errors; eslint, prettier clean |
| B79 | Medium | A CSV column with a blank header lost its data on import | Fixed, [#692](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/692) | `recordsOf` dropped every blank-header column | Such a column with data is named `Column N`; only empty ones are dropped | `csv.test.ts` "names a column with no name but something in it…" (failed before); import 519 passed; svelte-check 0 errors; eslint, prettier clean |
| B80 | Medium | An Evernote attachment named with `#` or `%` was imported but its link reached nothing | Fixed, [#695](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/695) | `pointAt` encoded spaces only | It uses the import's own link encoder (`encodeTarget`) | `evernote.test.ts` (failed before); import 519 passed; svelte-check 0 errors; eslint, prettier clean |
| B81 | Medium | Video, audio and embedded files in an HTML, Notion or Evernote import were copied but the note had no link to them | Fixed, [#696](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/696) | `from-html` drops those elements as page chrome | An opt-in `mediaLinks` writes them as links (through the same link checks); the import readers turn it on | `from-html.test.ts`, `plain.test.ts` (failed before); markdown 1209 passed, tsc clean; import 519 passed; svelte-check 0 errors; eslint, prettier clean |
| B82 | Medium | A CSV attached to a Notion page became a database and a note per row, and the CSV itself was not imported | Fixed, [#698](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/698) | Every CSV in a Notion export was read as a database | Only a CSV whose name carries a Notion id is a database | `notion.test.ts` "a CSV attached to a page is the file it was…" (failed before); import 519 passed; svelte-check 0 errors; eslint, prettier clean |
| B83 | Medium | Renaming a folder lost the board order of every database further inside it | Fixed, [#684](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/684); the first fix looked in a tree from before the move and the app hands one from after (third re-audit), now fixed on the same branch | `orderMoved` read only the moved folder's own note and the notes above it | The notes of every folder under the moved one are read too | `order-paths.test.ts` "…the databases a renamed folder holds, however deep" (failed before); desktop views/workspace 1000 passed; svelte-check 0 errors |
| B84 | Low | A database named `...md` was written into the folder above, over a note of that name | Fixed, [#688](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/688) | `.md` stripped left `..`, which the name cleaning kept | A name of dots alone falls back to the default | `new-database.test.ts` (failed before); views 147 passed; svelte-check 0 errors |
| B97 | Medium | "Add task" from Shortcuts aborted the whole running app when the inbox note had both line endings (final re-audit) | Fixed, [#699](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/699) | `appended` rebuilt the offset after the last words from trimmed lengths and one assumed line ending, so `&note[..kept]` ran past the note or off a character, and `panic = "abort"` | The offset is counted on the note's own lines | `siri.rs` `a_task_lands_after_the_last_words_of_a_note_of_mixed_endings` (macOS CI; test pushed alone first) |
| B100 | Medium | Setting or removing a front-matter key whose `\|` or `>` value held a blank line left the rest of the value behind, read as more of the key above (final re-audit; incomplete earlier fix) | Fixed, [#700](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/700) | `withUnder` ended a key's value at the first blank line | A blank line is part of the value where more of the value follows | `front-matter.test.ts` "takes a value of paragraphs whole…" (failed before); markdown 1210 passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B101 | Medium | Renaming a board option rewrote the same word for every other property in a filter (`priority == "Low"` became `"Backlog"` too) (final re-audit) | Fixed, [#702](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/702) | `renamedGroup` swapped the quoted word throughout any filter that named the property | The filter is parsed and only literals compared with that property, or passed to its `contains`, change; a filter that does not parse is left alone | `edit.test.ts` "only comparisons of the renamed property change…" (failed before); views 146 passed, bases 802 passed; svelte-check 0 errors, tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B102 | High | When an editor deleted their account, the PDF behind a page note they made in the owner's space went with it, and every device opened blank sheets (final re-audit) | Fixed, [#701](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/701) | `handedOn` hands the owner pictures, files and chat files, but never read `notes.paper` | The space's papers only the leaving account holds are handed to the owner too | `account.test.ts` "a paper it hung on a page note in a space that stays…" (failed before); sync 84 files passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B103 | Medium | An editor in one space could read a PDF the owner keeps elsewhere by naming its hash as a page note's paper (final re-audit; same class as B4) | Fixed, [#703](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/703) | `PUT /notes/:id/paper` took any blob the owner holds, wherever it lives | The hash must be the asker's own, or the owner's and already in this space | `papers.test.ts` "cannot name bytes the owner keeps for something outside this space" and three more (failed before); sync 84 files passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B104 | Medium | Deleting an account left its feedback reports, email included, in the GitHub issues they became, and a report erased while its issue was opening still opened one (final re-audit) | Fixed, [#704](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/704) | The erase deleted the rows and told GitHub nothing; `openIssue` never looked again after its POST | Opened issues are closed with a neutral title and body, retried nightly as leftovers; an issue opened during an erase is withdrawn at once | `feedback-issues.test.ts` "a report whose account is deleted" (3 tests, failed before); sync 84 files passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B113 | Medium | The same as B103 for v2 file entries: an editor could point a file in one space at bytes the owner keeps for another and download them (final re-audit) | Fixed, [#705](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/705) | `blobSizes` (`sync2/ops.ts`) and the replace check in `PUT /v2/files` took any blob the owner holds | One rule with #703's: the asker's own bytes, or the owner's already in this space | `sync2-files.test.ts` "the bytes an editor names" (two failed before); sync 2167 passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B118 | Medium | One side replacing a word while the other inserted one before it merged clean as glued words (`Call Ann on Mondaynext .`) (second re-audit; older than round 2) | Fixed, [#706](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/706), stacked on #694 | `written()` ordered an insertion at a replacement's start like two insertions at one point, by the CRDT order, so the inserted words ran into the replacement's last word | At one point an insertion goes before a replacement | `diverge.test.ts` "a word put in before a word the other side replaced…" (4 cases, failed before); sync-core 218/222 passed, desktop sync 263; tsc, eslint, prettier clean; fuzz no case worse than `main`, #694 or the branch below |
| B119 | Medium | A line one side added under a line the other side extended took the other side's words (`- [ ] Pay rent today`) (second re-audit) | Fixed, [#709](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/709), stacked on #694 | For two insertions at one point `sameLine` answered before asking whether one breaks the line | That case asks `breaks()`; lines added after a line's own break still do not meet | `diverge.test.ts` "words added to a line the other side broke there stay on their own line" (3 failed before); sync-core 218/222 passed, desktop sync 263; tsc, eslint, prettier clean; fuzz no case worse than `main`, #694 or the branch below |
| B123 | High | A writer later made a reader who deleted their account took the pictures, files and papers they put into the owner's space with them (second re-audit; B102's class by another path) | Fixed, [#707](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/707) | `handedOn` read only spaces the leaver could still write in | Every space they are a member of is read; only what the space uses is handed over | `account.test.ts` "a picture it put into a space it may now only read…" (failed before); sync 2164 passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B124 | Medium | Imports wrote the same front-matter key twice (`date:` from Logseq's `created-at` and `date`; Notion's `Status` and `status`), which Obsidian refuses (second re-audit) | Fixed, [#711](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/711) (one branch for the three) | `frontMatterFor` pushed each folded key without looking at keys already written | `freeKey` steps a taken key aside (`date-2`) and table.ts uses it too | `meta.test.ts` "writes each key once…" (failed before); import 524 passed; svelte-check 0 errors; eslint, prettier clean |
| B125 | Medium | A Notion `Date` range, or a second date beside `Created`, was taken out of the note and written nowhere (second re-audit) | Fixed, [#711](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/711) (one branch for the three) | `metaOf` dropped a date line it could not read a day from, and `??=` dropped a second date (Notion Markdown and HTML, Logseq) | A date is the note's own only where a day reads and none came first; otherwise it is a property | `notion.test.ts` "a page dated twice, or with a range, keeps every date", `logseq.test.ts` (failed before); import 524 passed; svelte-check 0 errors; eslint, prettier clean |
| B126 | Medium | A Notion property named only with emoji or punctuation (`⭐`) was lost (second re-audit) | Fixed, [#711](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/711) (one branch for the three) | `propertyName` gave `''` and the row and the base column were skipped | Such a column is kept under its place (`column-N`), the same in rows and base | `meta.test.ts` "keeps a column whose name has nothing in it to keep…", `notion.test.ts` database test (failed before); import 524 passed; svelte-check 0 errors; eslint, prettier clean |
| B127 | High | A page note that did not parse opened as an empty plane, and the first stroke saved that over its pages, ink and paper (second re-audit) | Fixed, [#708](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/708) | `openDeck` refused only a failed read; the #534 guard was in `openCanvas` alone | `openDeck` asks `parseAhead` too | `naming.store.test.ts` "opening a page note whose file is not JSON" (failed before); desktop pages, canvas, workspace 1530 passed, store tests 263; svelte-check 0 errors |
| B128 | Medium | An automation's "set property" wrote its value into YAML as it came, so `Meeting: notes` became a mapping, `yes` a boolean and a newline closed the block; the agents' tools, a note's icon and its cover had the same fault (second re-audit; outside the diff) | Fixed, [#710](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/710) | `frontMatterEdit` takes YAML, and these callers handed it plain text | They write through `scalar`/`flowItem` (`writeProperty` for automations), as the property rows do | `acts.test.ts`, `workspace.test.ts`, `file-icon.test.ts`, `note-cover.test.ts`, `front-matter.test.ts` (failed before); desktop 235 passed in the touched dirs, markdown 1208; svelte-check 0 errors, tsc clean |
| B138 | High | A writer who left or was removed from a space, then deleted their account, took what they put into it with them; and bytes another account also held were never handed over, so they went when that account did too (third re-audit) | Fixed, [#718](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/718), stacked on #707 | The handover was keyed on memberships and on "nobody else holds it", not on which spaces use the bytes; pictures lived only in note bodies in R2 | A `note_pictures` table (migration 0059) kept on every save and by the nightly sweep; the handover asks every space not the leaver's for its files, papers, pictures and `spaces.files`, the old body read kept as the fallback | `account.test.ts` left, removed, a stranger's copy, a paper (failed before) and a picture taken out again; `blog.test.ts` sweep; sync 2173 passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B139 | Medium | A PDF moved, copied, restored or handed over to `X.pages.pdf` beside a blank `X.pages` became its paper, and deleting the note deleted the PDF for good (third re-audit; the other way round from B76) | Fixed, [#712](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/712) | `taken` and `paper_in_the_way` judged only a page note landing, never a PDF landing beside one | `paper_holder` names the page note a PDF would be the paper of; `taken`, `paper_in_the_way` and `copied` ask it, so a move or copy is refused and a restore or handover steps aside; `freeName` likewise | `paths.rs`, `notes.rs`, `trash.rs`, `handed_pdfs.rs` tests (failed on all three CI systems before; tests pushed alone first), `pdf-names.store.test.ts`; rust CI green; svelte-check 0 errors |
| B140 | Medium | A clean merge ticked a task nobody ticked: a tick on one side and a task added above it on the other gave `- [x] eggs` (third re-audit) | Fixed on [#694](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/694)'s branch, with the prose cases the third re-audit found #694 had broken | `read()` diffed "one line gone, two added" by character, so the old box became the new task's; and its semantic cleanup over the whole run slid an insertion onto a space the other side deleted (`nowcall`) | `paired()` reads such a run as each line beside its most similar new version; the cleanup is held to the changed middle; an insertion is placed where it cuts no word; `glued()` makes edits meet that would run two words together; the #530 repair may not drop a word only one side wrote | `diverge.test.ts` tick, glued-words and a 500-run ticks property (failed before); fuzz: 0 merges worse than `main` or the stack before, line and prose, four seeds; sync-core 232 passed, desktop sync 263 |
| B141 | High | A canvas or page note left open at quit whose file stopped parsing came back blank on relaunch, and the first stroke saved that over it (third re-audit) | Fixed, [#715](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/715) | The #534 and B127 guard was on the open paths, not on `drafted` in the session restore | `drafted` refuses such a file as its open does | `unreadable-restore.effect.test.ts` (failed before); restore and store tests 274 passed; svelte-check 0 errors |
| B142 | Medium | "New page" on a board, and forms, failed for a property whose name is not ASCII (`priorität`), and lost the typed title (third re-audit) | Fixed, [#714](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/714) | The agent key rule `KEY` was ASCII only, and `BoardNew` cleared the title before the write | The rule takes Unicode letters, marks and digits with blanks, dots and hyphens between, refusing only what front matter would not read back as the key; a failed write puts the title back | `agent.test.ts` (failed before); bases 803 passed, views 145; tsc, svelte-check 0 errors |
| B143 | Medium | "Add property" with a name the note already has emptied that key (`tags: ''`) (third re-audit) | Fixed, [#719](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/719) | `finishAdding` wrote `''` under a name the note already had, in any case | `addProperty` refuses a taken name; the field shows it taken, as a name field does, and a commit goes to that row | `property-edits.test.ts` (failed before); markdown 1210 passed, `PropertiesPanel.test.ts`; svelte-check 0 errors |
| B144 | Medium | An import put a second front-matter block over a note's own (Bear, Logseq) (third re-audit) | Fixed, [#720](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/720) | `noteText` always put the export's block and the heading before the body, whatever the body opened with | A note's own block stays the only one; the export's keys go into it where the note lacks them, and the heading goes below | `meta.test.ts`, `bear.test.ts`, `logseq.test.ts`, `front-matter.test.ts` (failed before); import 522 passed, markdown 1209; svelte-check 0 errors |
| B145 | Medium | Import tags were dropped or merged (`🔥` gone, `C++` and `C#` both `C`) (third re-audit) | Fixed, [#717](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/717) | `tagName` had its own narrower rule and folded away what it could not hold | Tags follow `TAG_NAME`; a label that cannot be one keeps its words under `labels:` (stacked on #711 for `freeKey`) | `meta.test.ts` "keeps every tag the app can hold…" (failed before); import 526 passed; svelte-check 0 errors |
| B146 | Medium | Importing a database twice stepped the note aside (`Tasks 2.md`) and not its folder, so the new database showed nothing and its pages landed in the old folder (third re-audit) | Fixed, [#713](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/713) | `restamped` gave each file a free name on its own | A folder note and its folder are named together, outermost first, and what is under the folder follows | `apply.test.ts` "a folder note and its folder step aside together" and one more (failed before); import 520 passed; svelte-check 0 errors |
| B147 | Medium | Emptying a deleted space for good kept every note's words in the search index (third re-audit) | Fixed, [#716](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/716) | `purgeSpace` never deleted `note_search` rows | They go in the same batch as the notes | `trash.test.ts` "a space now keeps no words of its notes to search by" (failed before); sync 2164 passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| B161 | Medium | A folder named `Report.pages` (a Pages package) took a separate `Report.pages.pdf` beside it along when it was moved, renamed, copied or trashed, and a purge of the trash deleted the PDF (fourth re-audit) | Fixed, [#725](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/725), stacked on #721 | Paper-following code tested `is_pages` without `is_file` | `holds_paper` (a page note that is a file) in `move_held`, `copy_held` and `paper_in_the_way`; a restore no longer deletes a paper that failed to move back | `paths.rs` `a_folder_of_a_page_notes_name_holds_no_paper` (failed on all three CI systems before); rust CI green |
| B162 | High | A former writer could delete the pictures, papers and files they once put into the owner's space through `DELETE /v1/blobs/:hash`, for good (fourth re-audit) | Fixed, [#722](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/722), stacked on #718 | `letGo` deleted the object once no `blobs` row named it | `usedElsewhere` (shared with erase) finds spaces of others that use the bytes; letting go hands the row to their owners and the object goes only when nobody uses it | `blobs.test.ts` removed writer giving back a picture and a paper (failed before); sync `pnpm test` and tsc green; eslint, prettier clean |

| B164 | Medium | A note or space emptied for good kept its task reminders, with the task's words, until their day (fourth re-audit) | Fixed, [#726](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/726) | No purge path deleted `push_reminders`, which has no foreign key | `forgetReminders` on a note's purge, per chunk in a space's; migration 0061 clears rows of notes already gone | `push.test.ts` note and space purge, 0061 test (failed before); sync `pnpm test` and tsc green; eslint, prettier clean |
| B167 | High | Sync v2 read a canvas or page note another program left unreadable (cut short, conflict markers) as an empty plane and sent every node out as deleted, to every device (fourth re-audit; older than round 2) | Fixed, [#723](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/723) | `foldIn` turned the document to `readCanvas(file)`, which reads text that is not a canvas as one with nothing on it | `readable()` asks `canvasIn`; such text is left for its program to finish | `engine.test.ts` "another program's unreadable canvas…" (failed before); desktop sync2 and sync 264 passed; svelte-check 0 errors |
| B168 | Medium | An imported note opening with a rule (`---`, as the HTML importers write `<hr>`) lost its intro into the front matter (fourth re-audit; regression from #720) | Fixed on [#720](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/720)'s branch | `noteText` trusted any `---` pair as the note's own block | `ownBlock` takes a block as the note's own only where its lines are keys | `meta.test.ts` "a rule at the top of a note is a rule, not properties" (failed before); import 523 passed |
| B169 | Medium | Keys #714 lets the board write (`priorität`, `a.b`) turned the properties panel into source, and `note.status` was written as a twin of `status` (fourth re-audit; regression from #714) | Fixed on [#714](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/714)'s branch | Four key rules disagreed: `properties.ts` `KEY`/`NESTED` read ASCII keys only, and `note.status` was written as a key | One `isPropertyName` beside front matter's `KEY`, used by the rows, renames, the bases tools and the agents; `note.x` is written as `x` | `properties.test.ts`, `property-edits.test.ts`, `agent.test.ts`, `workspace.test.ts` (failed before); markdown 1211, bases 803, editor 1650 passed, desktop 489 in the touched dirs; tsc, svelte-check 0 errors |
| B170 | Medium | #694's line pairing made merges of long rewritten lines up to a thousand times slower (a 400-line paste: 25 s against 0.4 s) on the window's thread (fourth re-audit) | Fixed on [#694](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/694)'s branch, merged up into #706 and #709 | `paired()` scored every pair with an unclocked diff of lines up to 1,000 characters, twice, and read a run that paired nothing twice | A length bound and the common ends first; only middles up to 400 characters, 4,000 a run, compared by letter; every score kept; similarity as the share of both lines in common (which also fixes the CRLF glue) | `diverge.test.ts` "a line replaced by a paste of long lines…" under 2.5 s (5.6 s before); timings within noise of `main`; fuzz 0 new bad merges; sync-core 233 passed, desktop sync 263 |

### Round 2 status (closed 2026-10-24)

Stopped on request after the twenty-second re-audit, without a further one: every
Critical, High and Medium that the re-audits found has a fix, and no new search was
made after that. Twenty-two complete re-audits ran; each found new Mediums, most in
links across spaces, escapes in plain text and the 'ask' clashes of sync, and many of
those were regressions of the round before. So this says what was found and fixed,
not that nothing is left.

| Severity | Found | Fixed | Open |
| --- | --: | --: | --: |
| Critical | 2 | 2 | 0 |
| High | 50 | 50 | 0 |
| Medium | 191 | 190 | 1 (B182, skipped) |
| Low | 136 | 24 | 112 |

The skipped list (B95, B114, B182, B191, B192, B212) holds what could not be fixed or
verified here; it counts with the Lows. B324 was withdrawn. The last full suite passed
everywhere but `services/machine`'s `linux.test.ts`, whose 14 tests need node-pty,
which does not build on this machine; Rust and Android are verified by CI only.

All fixes land together through one integration branch, which merges every PR's head
and resolves their conflicts once; the first-paint weight and the bundle's code budget
are raised there for the sum of them.

## Round 3

Started 2026-10-24 from `main` at `b501fa0e1` (after #950), with the twenty-third
re-audit. Each fixed bug has its own branch (`claude/burn-down-r3-bNNN`) and pull
request off `main`.

Same method and severity scale as rounds 1 and 2.

| # | Severity | Bug | Status | Root cause | Fix | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| B449 | Medium | A task ending in a code span that holds ` #x` read the `#x`` as a trailing tag, so the text kept an open backtick, editing the words wrote `run `git tag extra #v2` #v2` and the tag could not be removed | Fixed, [#956](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/956) | No tag reader consulted code spans: B435 made the field readers skip code but `trailingTokens` still took `AT_END.tag`, whose character class allows a backtick, from inside a span | `trailingTokens` refuses a tag match that starts inside `codeSpans(head)`, the same skip `fieldAtEnd` does | Regression tests in task-line, task-edits and the desktop task-edit chain (prefillFor, changeOf) fail before and pass after; markdown 1317, bases 809 and desktop 12234 tests pass, tsc, svelte-check, prettier and eslint clean |
| B450 | Medium | Tags inside inline code mid-line were read as tags (`run `a #foo` now` gave `foo`), and removing the chip cut ` #foo` out of the user's code; the search index counted them too | Fixed, [#956](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/956) | Same root: `tagsIn`, `tagsAmongWords` and the search index's `readInline` (and its Rust twin `read_inline`) matched `#name` without asking whether it stood in code, while the renderer tokenizes code first | One rule, code spans are never tags: `codeAndWords`, `codeSpans` and `inCode` moved from escapes.ts to tags.ts (so the first paint gains no module) and every tag reader skips matches inside a span; the search index cuts a note into blocks (paragraph, list item, heading, ended by a blank line, a fence or a line that opens a block) and each block into code spans, so a span that runs across a paragraph's soft line break is read as CommonMark reads it, in search/tags.ts (`blocksOf`) and in tags.rs (`blocks`, with spans computed only for a block that holds a `#`); the weight budget was raised to 3,354,000 with a note | Regression tests in task-line, task-edits and search tags.test.ts (including spans across lines and across block ends), mirrored in tags.rs, fail before and pass after; markdown 1317 and desktop 12252 tests pass, tsc, svelte-check, prettier and eslint clean; tags.rs is rustfmt-clean and clippy-pedantic-clean standalone, its tests run in CI |
| B451 | Medium | A `.txt` checklist (Simplenote, Drafts, nvALT) with no heading, link or bold imported with its boxes escaped, so `- [ ] milk` became a bullet with literal brackets, and `~~struck~~` and `_em_` lost their marks | Fixed, [#954](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/954) | `MARKDOWN_SAYS` in `import/plain.ts` had no pattern for a task item, a strike or underscore emphasis, so such a file was taken for plain text and `plainWords` escaped the brackets, tildes and underscores | Added three patterns to `MARKDOWN_SAYS`: a task item (bulleted or numbered with `.` or `)`, empty or not), and a `~~word~~` and an `_word_` pair bounded on both sides by a blank, a bracket, a quote or punctuation, the underscore one with two characters at least so a ` _o_ ` cheer and snake case stay plain; a quoted task was already caught by the quote pattern, and inline code and a table delimiter row were left out because `plainWords` keeps them unchanged, so they would only widen the L1 risk | Regression tests for a checklist, nested, empty, numbered and quoted tasks, strikes and underscores in quotes and brackets, plus plain-text cases (snake case, tildes inside words, `~~5~~10`, a cheer, `[a]`, `[ ]` without a blank) that still escape `$5`; import tests 596/596, svelte-check 0 errors, prettier and eslint clean, full desktop vitest green apart from one unrelated timing test under load that passes alone |
| B452 | Medium | A note waiting on a clash, renamed while the account was away and answered while still away, was sent up as a new note under its new name and the note itself deleted, the other device's newer words with it | Fixed, [#957](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/957) | The protection lasted only as long as the clash: a rename that could not be told left the entry at the old name, `settle` told it once more and let go, and the push then read the new name as untracked and the old one as a delete | Every rename that does not land is written down on the mirror (`untold`, by id, kept across launches and handed to the v2 first pass) and told again at the start of the next pull: with the account's words while the question waits, by the live clash ids, and the file's once answered. Later renames follow it, a rename made on another device meanwhile takes the account's name, and a refusal for good or five refusals let it go by forgetting the entry rather than deleting the note. Until then pull and push leave both names alone | Regression tests in sync/mirror.test.ts (renamed and answered away, renamed twice away and read back, refused then told again, refused for good and too often, renamed on another device too) and services/sync/test/sync2-migrate.test.ts (a held note v1 renamed and could not tell) fail without the fix and pass; desktop vitest 12254 passed; sync service vitest 2303 passed; svelte-check 0 errors; prettier and eslint clean; first-paint budget still met |
| B453 | Medium | A new note made at the name a held note was renamed away from while the account was away was sent up as the held note, so the other device's copy became the unrelated note | Fixed, [#957](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/957) | Same root as B452: the entry stayed at the old name, and the push finds notes by path, so the new file there matched the held note's entry; the v2 first pass matched it the same way by place | Same fix: the untold rename is told before anything else in the pull, which re-keys the entry and asks the question again at the new name, so the new file goes up as a note of its own; while it is still untold the push skips the file at the old name, and the v2 first pass matches the note to its new name and keeps it out of matching by place | Regression tests in sync/mirror.test.ts (a new note at the freed name) and sync2-migrate.test.ts fail without the fix and pass, with the fake account now giving a second note at a reused path its own id; same suites, typecheck, prettier and eslint as B452 |
| B454 | Medium (security) | Any web page or app could start or stop a recording, open a note or tick a task on Android, because the exported MainActivity took the command, open, tick and reminder extras from any intent, including an `intent://` link with the BROWSABLE `nib` filter | Fixed, [#953](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/953) | `read()` in MainActivity.kt trusted every extra it was handed, and B442 only narrowed the command ids to those tiles send, which include `record` | Tiles, both widgets and the reminder notification wrap their intent in `MainActivity.sealed`, which adds a per-install word kept in private prefs. `read()` takes command, open, tick and reminder extras only when that word matches; shares and nib:// links are untouched | New android.test.ts test failed before and passes after; android.test.ts 34/34, svelte-check 0 errors, prettier and eslint clean on the test file; full vitest 12200 passed, the only failures are unrelated timing tests in import (tomboy 2.6 s vs 2 s under load); Kotlin compile left to CI android-check |
| B455 | Medium (security) | Importing a document or exporting a deck could write a crafted picture anywhere on the machine through pandoc 2.15 to 3.1.5 | Fixed, [#952](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/952) | `--extract-media` was passed to every pandoc, but before 3.1.6 a picture whose name hides `../` in percent-encoding (CVE-2023-35936, or double-encoded, CVE-2023-38745) is written outside the media folder; `--sandbox` does not cover it, so a docx with such a picture name got out of the sandboxed import and a data: URI got out of the unsandboxed deck export; `read_refusal` only refused pandoc older than 2.15 | `copying` has pandoc copy both advisory pictures four folders deep in a temporary folder of its own, with a 10 second limit, and trusts it only when nothing but folders turns up outside the media folder anywhere in that tree; an import on any other pandoc runs without `--extract-media` and returns `leftOut`, which the window shows when the document had pictures, and a revealjs deck is refused; a try that could not be made (no pandoc, no folder, a failed or hung pandoc) says so instead of asking for 3.1.6. A try rather than a version check, because Debian 12's 2.17 and Ubuntu 24.04's 3.1.3+ds-2 carry the fixes without a new number | By hand on real binaries: upstream 3.1.3 lets the single-encoded picture out, 3.1.4 the double-encoded one, 3.1.6 and Ubuntu's 3.1.3+ds-2 neither, and the sandboxed docx import agrees on all four; tests with a shell standing in for pandoc (writes outside however deep or by link: not trusted; writes inside or makes folders: trusted; exits non-zero, missing, hung: an answer of its own within the limit), a docx import exploit test and a deck exploit test that check the whole folder, the picture and deck tests for both branches; svelte-check 0 errors, vitest export/importing 445 passed, prettier and eslint clean, CI check green on all three rust rows |
| B456 | Medium | One timed-out frame word turned off the guarded bridge calls for the whole launch: on a slow cold start the share that started the app was lost, widget rows stopped updating, reminders were never handed to the alarms, reminder Done presses were not taken and AI keys read as missing or looked saved when they were not | Fixed, [#955](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/955) | `frameWord` in mobile/bridge.ts kept a timed-out `''` for the whole launch, and its callers took a call made with `''`, which the activity drops without a word, for one that worked | `frameWord` still answers within 2 s but never keeps `''`: the next caller asks again, a late answer is kept, an empty answer or a throwing ask is no word, and `wordSaid()` settles once the word is said. The key calls throw `NoWord` instead of writing, forgetting or reading with `''` (the pane and the vault treat that as not yet known, not as no key, and look again once the word is said); `hand` throws and the scheduler forgets the plan it did not hand, so it is handed again, and start.svelte.ts re-plans and takes the presses once the word is said; the widget rows are pushed again then; dictation turns itself off rather than say it listens | New tests for the bridge, the keys, the reminders' hand and presses, the scheduler, the widget rows and dictation each failed before and pass after; the desktop suite (884 files), svelte-check (0 errors), prettier and eslint pass, and android.test.ts and the launch budget follow the change |
| B457 | Low | Twenty-third re-audit, views and packages: a `.txt` with any line like `# of items: 5` imports as markdown in full, so that line becomes a heading, `$5-$10` maths and `<b>x</b>` HTML; clearing the words of `- [ ] Buy #milk now` writes `- [ ]  #milk` with a double blank; a kept tag put back lands after newly added ones (`Buy now #new #milk`); an escaped `\#b` copied from the reading view pastes back as a live tag, and a `%%comment%%` is dropped on that round trip; `[x:: [[N]]]` reads the value `[[N` and leaves `]]` in the text; pandoc export runs without `--sandbox`, so a shared note's `![](/abs/path)` or `../..` picture could be embedded in the ODT or DOCX or copied into `Deck_files` (unverified); a pandoc import extracts pictures into `media/` beside the source, outside the space, and two imports from one folder can overwrite each other's (unverified); an edited Save in the task field closes double blanks (`Buy #milk  now`) | Open, accepted | Per item | - | Code reading and probes |
| B458 | Low | Twenty-third re-audit, sync: answering a clash during a held rename's round trip writes 'theirs' to the old name, which goes up as a duplicate, and the answered question comes back; a waiting clash is not dropped when the account's copy later matches the file, so 'theirs' afterwards writes the stale words over the agreed copy (kept as a version); a rule changed from 'ask' to 'newest' or 'both' while a clash waits settles a newer copy by the new rule, and 'theirs' then writes the clash's older words over it (kept as a version); on Windows `follow` for a folder move mixes separators, so push may miss the held note while offline (unverified); settling a clash whose note followed a rename undoes a rename the other device made meanwhile; the browser build's `TextDecoder` drops a UTF-8 BOM and replaces bad bytes, so a `.base` or sidecar read back through `read_asset` hashes differently and goes up once more; `blobSizes` and the paper check still count a deleted `kind='file'` row as already in the space (as in B432) | Open, accepted | Per item | - | Code reading |
| B459 | Low | Twenty-third re-audit, Rust and rest: the browser build's `save_asset` checks only `assets`, so dropping the same `.canvas`, `.md` or `.base` again always makes `x 2.canvas`; its non-fatal `TextDecoder` mangles a binary-plist `.webloc` (kept as bytes before B441) and strips a `.base`'s BOM; `delete_note` and `taken` look for `X.pdf.highlights.json` only in `files`, so a sidecar an older build left in `assets` survives the PDF's delete or rename and later returns as a new PDF's highlights; `writeBytes` removes the old asset row before `writeNote`, so a failed write loses the old words; a revealjs export overwrites files already in `<name>_files` without saying so; `askExactAlarms` has no frame-word check, so a framed page can open the exact-alarm settings over and over | Open, accepted | Per item | - | Code reading |

B440 is in no released build (v0.18.0 is 19c63cf42, edge and mobile-edge are 6efaa0e9b on unstable, none contains #950; only a source build of main such as AUR orden-git since 2026-10-24 could hold its writes), so no old content is migrated | Regression test with bullets, numbers and a typed escape, plus a fuzz of 4000 comments and descriptions across six note shapes (CRLF, tabs, nested, headings, a code block after) checking exact read-back and that the whole note's task list, parents and sections are unchanged; the auditor's 20k comment fuzz went from 10279 changed task lists to 0 |

A fence left open is written `\```` (one more backslash, taken off on read), so it no longer hides every later task, link and heading of the note from scan.ts and links.rs (this also closes a23 L2 for descriptions). Main's B440 writes are in no released build, as for B462, so no migration | Regression tests that fenced lines are written verbatim, read back, rewrite to nothing and add no task or comment, an open fence and a typed `\```` round trip escaped, and a tab-indented CRLF note keeps its endings; the fuzz above includes open, escaped and over-indented fences; the auditor's 20k description fuzz is now exact with no task list change (was 124, all open fences) |

### Round 3 status (closed 2026-10-25)

Stopped on request after the twenty-third re-audit, the one complete re-audit of the
round, made in five areas (views and packages, sync, Rust and rest, desktop, and the
gap areas). It found 15 Mediums (B449-B456, B461-B465, B467, B468), two of them
security (B454, B455); all are fixed and merged. Each fix was reviewed once by an
independent agent and reworked on its findings. No new bug search and no further
review was made after the reworks, so the reworks themselves were not reviewed again.
So this says what was found and fixed, not that nothing is left.

Every fix PR (#952 to #960) is merged into main by squash, the last as `8aba13b69`.
The first-paint weight budget was raised once more in #956, to 3,355,700, for the sum
of the fixes. The full suite on main at `8aba13b69` passed everywhere but
`services/machine`'s `linux.test.ts`, whose 14 tests need node-pty, which does not
build on this machine (desktop 12298 passed, sync 2303, markdown 1317, bases 809);
Rust and Android are verified by CI only.

| Severity | Found | Fixed | Open |
| --- | --: | --: | --: |
| Critical | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| High | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Medium | 15 | 15 | 0 |
| Low | 6 | 0 | 6 |

The Lows are six rows of many items each (B457-B460, B466, B469), all open and
accepted.

## Round 4

Started 2026-10-26 from `main` at `9ae664c33` (after #951), with the twenty-fourth
re-audit. Each fixed bug has its own branch (`claude/burn-down-r4-bNNN`) and pull
request off `main`; related bugs share one.

Same method and severity scale as rounds 1 to 3.

| # | Severity | Bug | Status | Root cause | Fix | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| B470 | Medium | A new note made at the name a note was renamed away from, and renamed itself before the first rename was told, took the renamed note's id, history and words, and the renamed note went up as a new note (B453 not fully fixed) | Fixed, [#963](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/963) | `movedHere` in sync/pass.ts picked the notes to move by entry path, and an untold rename's entry stays at the old name, so the file now at that name was taken for the note; `movedOne` wrote its words into it and `told` dropped the untold record | `movedHere` leaves out an entry with an untold rename unless the move is that very rename (`to === untold.to`), for one file and for every note under a moved folder | Three new tests in sync/mirror.test.ts (during an outage, offline, online) fail before and pass after; mirror.test.ts 110 passed, desktop vitest 12305 passed, sync2-migrate 17 passed, svelte-check 0 errors, prettier and eslint clean |
| B471 | Medium | A rename made during a short server outage came back as two notes: five 5xx answers, counted even in passes whose `changes` failed, let the rename go, so the old name was written back and the new one went up as a new note | Fixed, [#963](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/963) | `untoldBy` counted every `ApiError`, 5xx included, as the account refusing the rename, and `toldAgain` runs before `api.changes`, so each failed pass cost a try | `untoldBy` sorts the answer: a network error leaves the record as it is; 5xx, 408 and 429 add to `failing {since, times}` and let go only an hour after the first and after five failures at least; 409 counts tries (five, then let go); any other 4xx lets go at once. The pull reads on past a page with an untold note but stops moving `mirror.cursor` from that page (`holding`), so later pages are read and the page is read again once told. The first fix (count only status < 500) had no cap at all; the review's Lows led to this rework | Tests for a 408, 429 and 503 outage that the rename survives, a 503 that never clears and is let go after an hour, and later pages read while one is held, failed before and pass after; same suites as B470; CI green |
| B472 | Medium | A queued message taken into the field to change was stranded in the queue when the edit was let go (Escape, Cancel, another thread) after the answer had ended, and a later send ran ahead of it (B465 rework regression) | Fixed, [#965](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/965) | The edit state (`editing`, `requeue`, `aside`) was ended per path, and `cancelEdit` spliced the message back into a queue without asking whether that queue would still run | `owed` marks a thread whose answer ended with nothing to send while its queued message was in the field; `cancelEdit` gives the field back, puts the message back and drains an owed queue (live, unarchived threads only); `show` lets the edit go and drains the left thread after `this.open` changes. Review of the first fix found a Medium regression, fixed before merge: deleting or archiving such a chat sent the message and wrote the deleted chat back to disk; delete now drops the held message and archive puts it back in its queue | New chat.test.ts cases (Escape and thread switch after the run ended, delete, archive) fail before and pass after; desktop vitest 886 files, 12319 passed; svelte-check 0 errors, prettier and eslint clean; CI green |
| B473 | Medium | A queued message being changed and then steered (Ctrl+Enter) or sent as a slash command kept the edit open, so Escape, Cancel or a thread switch queued its old words again and they were sent twice (B465 rework regression) | Fixed, [#965](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/965) | Only `submit` and `cancelEdit` ended the edit; `steer` and the Composer's `runTyped` and `pick` emptied the field and left `requeue` and `aside` set | One `taken()` ends the edit wherever the field's words leave it: submit, steer, a typed or a picked command | chat.test.ts steer and command cases and Composer.effect.test.ts (typed and picked commands call `chat.taken()`) fail before and pass after; same suites as B472 |
| B474 | Medium | Pressing the pencil on a second queued message while one was in the field lost the first; the pencil on an earlier message while a queued one was held lost the queued one and its changes (#960 regression) | Fixed, [#965](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/965) | A second `editQueued` overwrote `requeue` and the field, and `startEdit` let `submit` null `requeue` | A second `editQueued` puts the held message back first, as changed; `startEdit` puts it back unsent, to go after the earlier edit or on Escape | chat.test.ts "second pencil" and "pencil on an earlier message" fail before and pass after; same suites as B472 |
| B475 | Medium | An agent call naming both `tab` and `space` wrote the tab's relative path into the named space, creating a note there or overwriting one with no `if_rev` check | Fixed, [#964](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/964) | `namedTab` read the tab's path inside its own space, but `noteOf`, `pdfOf` and `canvasOf` recursed with `call.args.space` kept, so the path was resolved against the named space | `tabsOwnSpace` in tab-target.ts refuses (`bad_arguments`) a `space` beside a tab unless it is the tab's own, for every file tab (notes, PDFs, canvases) and in the draft branches of notes.ts and canvas.ts | workspace.test.ts "a tab and a space named together" (six note verbs) fails before and passes after; the reviewer probed the PDF, canvas and draft paths; desktop vitest 12305 passed, pnpm check 0 errors, prettier and eslint clean |
| B476 | Medium | Renaming or moving a folder rewrote no links to the notes and files inside it, so path-qualified wikilinks, markdown links and canvas file cards into it went dead | Fixed, [#969](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/969) | `retargetIn` in link-index.svelte.ts picked only links that resolved to `was` exactly with `nameOf(was)` as their last segment, and a folder is never a link target; it also resolved only notes and tab files, never pictures | `retargetIn` maps each link's resolved target through the move by prefix (`moving`), notes inside a moved folder are written at their new path, files resolve through `resolveFile` too, and `stillReaches` keeps a link that still names its target. Review found five Mediums in the reworks, all fixed before merge: three main-thread stalls (every link scanning every file, 7.4 s at 1,000 notes; `byName` rebuilt per rewritten note, 51 s at 5,000; same-named files as in a Notion export) and two index races (a save during the pass undone in the index, and one before the pass reached the note left stale). Files are now indexed by whole folded path, the rewritten notes are told to the index once after the pass with the entry read right after their words, and the pass breathes every 500 notes. The weight budget was raised to 3,374,300 | link-index.test.ts, file-ops.store.test.ts (folder rename and move with open notes, undo) and link-index.perf.effect.test.ts (compare counts and `byName` builds) fail before and pass after; desktop vitest 12377 passed; svelte-check 0 errors; prettier, eslint and knip clean; CI green |
| B477 | Medium | A note moved to another folder kept its own relative links and pictures, which then broke in the editor, the reading view and export | Fixed, [#969](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/969) | Same function: `points` picked only links that resolve to the moved note, so its own outgoing relative targets were never rebased | A note that changed folder rebases its relative markdown and picture targets from its new folder; wikilinks and bare names are left alone | link-index.test.ts "a note moved to another folder" fails before and passes after; same suites as B476 |
| B478 | Medium | Moving a folder holding archived notes into another space un-archived them on every device | Fixed, [#967](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/967) | The `moved` FileOp carried only the source root, so `Archive.moved` keyed the new path relative to the source space; outside it the key was absolute, `insideItsSpace` dropped it and the old key was restored | FileOp `moved` gains `into` (`rootHolding(to)`), which `keeping` passes on; `Archive.moved` takes the keys out of the source map and archives them in `into`'s map, relative to its root | archive.test.ts and file-ops.store.test.ts "a move into another space" fail before and pass after; the reviewer probed undo; desktop vitest 12299 passed (one tomboy timing test fails under load, passes alone), pnpm check 0 errors, prettier and eslint clean |
| B479 | Medium (security) | Export as OpenOffice or as a presentation put any local file (or a web picture) that a picture link named into the ODT or into `Deck_files`, so a note from a share could carry off `~/.ssh/id_rsa` | Fixed, [#968](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/968) | `convert` in pandoc.rs ran pandoc without `--sandbox` on the note's markdown, so pandoc fetched every picture target (absolute, `../`, any URL); `--sandbox` alone is no fix, because Debian and Ubuntu pandoc keep their data files on disk | Formats that carry pictures go source to a sandboxed `--to json`, then `vetted` walks the tree: a picture is admitted only when it canonicalizes to a file inside the note's space (no scheme, no `//` after decoding, no hidden folder, links followed) and is copied into a temporary folder under a plain name; every other one becomes its alt text and is reported missing; raw HTML sources are emptied for decks; then `--from json` with that folder as `--resource-path`. Other formats, epub and fb2 included, run with `--sandbox`. Review found a Medium in the rework, fixed before merge: a `data:` pass-through let `data:[/../..` reach any file on Windows; every `data:` link is refused again | Rust tests (an ODT and a deck whose picture lies outside the space, the refused links) run on real pandoc in CI's Linux row; 37 Rust tests and clippy pedantic in a scratch crate locally; CI green on every row |
| B480 | Medium | Importing a Word or ODT document wrote its pictures into `media/` beside the source, outside every space, so the new note's pictures were dead links and a second import from that folder overwrote them | Fixed, [#968](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/968) | `import_document` ran pandoc with `current_dir(beside)` and `--extract-media .`, and the page only opened the words as a draft | `import_from` extracts into a temporary folder and returns the pictures (24 MB each, 96 MB in all); `keepPictures` stores each with `storeBeside` for the planned note path and `relinked` repoints the links. Review found a Medium in the fix, fixed before merge: the pictures were written into a space shared to be read; now `pandocRoot` takes the sheet's root when it may be written, else `landing()`, the note opens in that same space, and with no writable space the import says the picture was lost | A Rust test that nothing is written beside the source; importing-read-only.test.ts (read-only space, removed root, no space yet) fails before and passes after; vitest import, export and workspace 1960 passed; svelte-check 0 errors |

### Round 4 status

The twenty-fourth re-audit, in five areas (sync, gap areas, desktop, Rust and rest,
and views), found 13 Mediums (B470-B482), one of them security (B479), and no
Critical or High. The fixes came with four more Mediums that were older than them:
B483 and B485 from the reviews of #965, B486 from the renderer Lows of the B482 fix
and its review, and B484 from the B478 fix's list of sibling stores. Each fix was
reviewed by an independent agent, reworked on its findings and reviewed again until
a review found no Critical, High or Medium; #969 took five reviews. Those reviews
caught nine Medium regressions in the fixes themselves (in #965, #968 twice, #969
five times and #972), all fixed before merge and folded into the rows above.

The PRs are merged into main by squash: #963 `436e9d166`, #964 `be5019b5b`, #965
`5fd08555d`, #966 `a5d0cf047`, #967 `5a844ba35`, #968 `90e30c588`, #969 `cef65f104`,
#970 `eb53c4e01`, #971 `ec29971b6`, #972 `fb1ba9599`. The first-paint weight budget was
raised several times as the fixes landed, from 3,355,700 to 3,374,300 on main.

Stopped 2026-10-27 on request without a final re-audit; full suite run on the integration branch (see the integration PR).

| Severity | Found | Fixed | Open |
| --- | --: | --: | --: |
| Critical | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| High | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Medium | 17 | 17 | 0 |
| Low | 6 | 0 | 6 |

The Lows are six rows of many items each (B487-B492), all open and accepted.

## Round 4b

The twenty-fifth re-audit ran on `main` at `ec29971b6` (after #971), complete, in six
areas: sync, Rust and rest, views, desktop, gap areas, and desktop2, a second desktop
pass over canvas-ops, pages-ops, terminal, settings and grants, web tabs, PDF, pages,
rows, palette and the Svelte components the first pass did not read. It was not
clean: 1 High (B495) and 23 Mediums (B493, B494, B496-B516). The reviews of the fixes
found four more Mediums older than them (B517-B520). Same branch, PR and review rule
as round 4.

The PRs merged into main by squash: #974 B497 `e69a1f43a`, #975 B495 `2b883384a`, #976
B493 and B494 `136dd3f93`, #977 B496 `fbaa6025d`, #978 B507-B510 `46330eb7a`, #979 B516
`4d2c38337`, #981 B505 and B506 `f49f25def`, #982 B517 `802e190e0`, #983 B501 and B502
`4a6f4f34e`, #984 B515 `0d9ab44de`, #985 B498-B500 `603eee7c5`, #987 B518 `2bb2a4272`,
#980 B503, B504, B519 and B520 `ad59f040c`. #986 (B511-B514) is open: its review found
a Medium in the fix, the rework is pushed, and its re-review and CI are pending.

Each fix was reviewed by an independent agent and reworked until a review found no
Critical, High or Medium, except the last rework of #980 (P1 of its third review) and
#986, which were not reviewed again. The reviews caught one High and fourteen Mediums in
the fixes themselves, all fixed before merge and folded into the rows above, and found
four older Mediums (B517-B520). The first-paint weight budget was raised as the fixes
landed, to 3,389,500 with #980.

The full suite on `ec29971b6` passed everywhere but the 14 node-pty tests of
`services/machine` (desktop 12382, sync 2303, editor 1655, markdown 1339, bases 809);
it has to run again once the round 4b fixes are in. Since the twenty-fifth re-audit
was not clean, a twenty-sixth is needed.

## Re-audit 26 and round 4c

The twenty-sixth re-audit ran on `main` at `affa7aa02` (after #988) in eight areas:
desktop, desktop2, desktop3 and desktop4 (the desktop files the first pass did not reach),
sync, views, tauri (Rust and rest) and gap areas. Web tabs were left out while #986 was
open. It was not clean: 1 High (B559) and 34 Mediums. B531 and B532 came from the reviews
of #980 and #988, B571 and B572 from the reviews of #995 and #1000, B568 and B570 from the
review of #1001 and its fix. B563 is the nightly drives workflow, red every night it ran;
B569 is three WebKit drives red on main. Same branch, PR and review rule as round 4.

From 2026-10-27 fixes land on `unstable` through one integration PR (branch
`claude/burn-down-integration`), never directly on `main`. The PRs squash-merged into
`main` before that are carried to `unstable` by the same integration PR.

| # | Severity | Bug | Status | Root cause | Fix | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

## Bug hunt 2026-11

Four rounds from 2026-11-02, each an audit by area (`findings/h1-*` to `h4-*`) and then one fix
branch per area (`claude/hunt-*`, `claude/hunt2-*`, `claude/hunt3-*`, `claude/hunt4-*`), merged
into one integration branch, `claude/hunt-round-1`, and carried to `unstable` by #1094. Audits
ran at `0dd38b11d`, `b0c3960d3`, `8db51019a` and `fa747cf3e`. Fixes were reviewed by independent agents and reworked;
the file reviews of rounds 3 and 4, and six rounds on hunt3-tauri, are kept in `findings/review*`.
What the reviews found in a fix is folded into its row; only bugs older than the hunt have their own.

### Round 1

| # | Severity | Bug | Status | Root cause | Fix | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| B585 | High | An agent's move of a nested AGENTS.md to the top of a space renamed the space's own AGENTS.md, and answered that it worked; the instructions became a plain note (H1-DESKTOP-1) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | `moveMany`'s AGENTS guard returns silently, then `movedIn` renames the name it assumes the note landed under | `movedIn` refuses onto any AGENTS spelling at the top and throws on `moveMany`'s false; its stop is never the instructions or taken | moved-in.store.test.ts; `b698dfa80` |
| B586 | Medium | `site:github.com orden`, `define:x` or `note: buy milk` in a web tab's address field did nothing on Enter (H1-DESKTOP-2) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | `SCHEME` read the first word as a scheme, so `webAddress` answered null | A known scheme or `//` is an address, else words; local paths and `user:pw@host` stay out of search | typed.test.ts "a word with a colon after it"; `592468cc3` |
| B587 | Medium | DOCX and ePub of a note with a control character held characters XML forbids, so strict readers refuse the file (H1-DESKTOP-3) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | The writers passed the characters on, in body, title and author | VT and FF become a space, the rest go, numeric references too | docx.test.ts, epub.test.ts "characters XML cannot carry"; `33e4ea5f1`; Word itself not run |
| B588 | Medium | TextBundle or Markdown export copied `![[photo.png]]`, `![a](photo(1).png)` and reference pictures but left text.md pointing elsewhere (H1-DESKTOP-4) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | `picturesIn` finds them; the rewrite did not reach those forms | Embeds, balanced parentheses, escaped sources and definitions go through the `picture` option | textbundle.test.ts "a picture however it was written"; `6e6f44674` |
| B589 | High | PNG and JPG of any note came out near-black with dark text, unreadable (H1-DESKTOP2-3) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | In an SVG image `:root` is the `<svg>`, so the tokens resolved dark under a light `<html>` | `svgOf` puts the page's `data-theme` on the svg root | image.test.ts "says the page's scheme on the picture's root"; drive export-looked-at.py reads pixels (base: paper 14,16,19); `fc9bdaa18` |
| B590 | Medium | A slow eraser pass across a pen line erased nothing, and a quick swipe skipped (H1-DESKTOP2-1) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | `erased` only dropped recorded points in reach, and the eraser was tested only at its events | `erased` cuts segments at the circle; `rubbedPath` covers the way between events, not where the plane moved | ink.test.ts "rubbing across a long straight segment"; pointer.test.ts "answers along the way", "walks no way the plane moved"; drive eraser-between.py; `3d93f5a0f` |
| B591 | Medium | Code block, Math, Table mid-line glued the words after the caret to the block, so a fence ran on to the next one; over a selection the words were replaced (H1-DESKTOP2-2) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | `insertBlock` did not end the block on its own line and replaced the selection | The block ends on its own line; fence, math and callout wrap a selection, the rest goes below the words | commands.test.ts "blocks"; drive block-on-its-lines.py; `3ef081c4b` |
| B592 | Medium | Export as PDF or Print within 1.2 s of an edit of a page note gave the old state, here one blank page (H1-DESKTOP2-4) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | `openTarget`'s `workspace.flush()` does not commit a plane | `workspace.wordsNow` (table cell, card, plane, rope) before the read, and a `owesWords` register in parting.ts | context.test.ts; drive export-looked-at.py; `fc9bdaa18` |
| B593 | Medium | A reload, quit or crash during an AI answer lost the message sent, the words so far and the record of what the agent changed (H1-DESKTOP2-5) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | `finish` was the first `keepThread` of a send | The thread is written as the answer begins, when a call finishes, at most 3 s apart and at parting; read back it ends in a Stopped row | chat.test.ts "an answer on its way"; threads.test.ts "a file written while its answer was on its way"; drive ai-answer-cut.py; `9b7662fa4`; real tool calls unit only |
| B594 | Medium | A connection lost mid-answer read as a finished answer; an empty answer showed nothing (H1-DESKTOP2-6) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | No finish reason and no `[DONE]` was not told apart from an end; anthropic and responses had the same hole | `Reader.finished` makes it an error that keeps the words, on all three wires; "The model did not answer." | engine.test.ts "a stream that stops before it says it is done", "an answer of nothing at all"; drive ai-answer-cut.py; `9b7662fa4`, `d8f187190` |

### Round 2

### Round 3

| B695 | Critical (security) | A page or a frame could answer the agent gate's checks and pay or act in a denied frame, unasked (review 2 of hunt3-tauri, older) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | `DOM.resolveNode` with no context, so FACTS, secret scan and frame address ran in the page's main world | `Page::object` resolves in nib's isolated world, one per frame; interface getters read past the instance | hunt3-tauri-live-final logs q1, q2, q4, q5; check.yml on 2fabfb4d9; regressions (same-site secret, frames unpressable) folded |
| B696 | High (security) | A press on what holds the button (`<p>`, `<li>`, a region) paid and deleted with no question, and submitted a sign-in (H3-TAURI-1, regression from round 2) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | Judged by the wrapper, while the press landed on the button | `aim` = `landing` + hit test; click, drag, upload, hover and tick judge the element at the point, and press that point; reviews: pseudo, pointer-events layer, what a mousemove changes | page-facts.test.ts; live; `280e9a18a`; review 1's Critical and two Highs folded |
| B697 | High (security) | A press on an iframe's own ref pressed inside it unjudged, on a denied site too (H3-TAURI-2) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | `lands_on` took an IFRAME as inside; nothing judged the frame's content | Judged inside; a denied frame by ref, point, drag, hover, upload: `site_denied` | Live: PRESSED-IN-DENIED 0 times; `280e9a18a`, `ca4bde071` |
| B698 | High (security) | A frame under a transform or zoom was judged at another point than the press; a scrollbar too (review 2, older; review 3) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | `frame_offset` took the first corner and no scale | `FrameMap` from the engine's quad, both ways; perspective refused | Live z1 to z5, c10; `FrameMap` unit tests |
| B699 | High (security) | A hit on a shadow root missed the host's listener: bare-text Lit components paid unasked (review 2, older) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | `ROUND_IT`, `PRESSED` had no `this.host` | A hit on a shadow root is its host | Live w1, w2 |
| B700 | High (security) | A page nested about 60 deep showed every secret in words and picture, and took 30 s per read (review 4, older in cause) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | `DOM.getDocument` depth -1 past serde_json's limit, reply dropped | `DOMSnapshot.captureSnapshot`, a flat reply; `UNREAD` secret fails closed | Live 70, 500, 1000 deep; unit test 1000 levels through the real parser |
| B701 | High (security) | A frame inside a cross-site frame was on no list: a card and a denied site readable in the picture (review 5, older) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | Auto-attach on the page's own session only | `cdp::follow_nested` on each frame session; cannot ask, or past 32 levels, refuses | Live t9, o2: 0 green; reader's tabs not run live |
| B702 | High (security) | A card field remounted during a snapshot wrote the number (review 5, older) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | Accessibility tree read before the capture | `gone_fields`: a value the later scan did not see is a secret; pictures scan before and after | Live 0 written at 5000 to 40000; a hostile 70 ms window stays open, see status |
| B703 | High | v2: one name of 256+ characters, from any shared writer, stopped sync in that space and the rest of the round, for every member, for good (H3-SYNC-1) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | `cleanPath` had no per-name limit, and `feedPageOf` answered null for the page | `cleanPath` holds names to `isName`; Worker and `feedPageOf` give a bad name the nearest good one (`heldName`); one throw no longer ends the round (`eachDue`) | test/bad-names.test.ts; wire.test; tree.test; test/unwritable.test.ts "a row a device cannot read" |
