| B705 | High | One unwritable note in a pull batch: it never arrived, and after a restart every note of the batch doubled on the account (H3-SYNC-3) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | The throw left `pullDocs` before `core.commit` | `project` catches a refusal (`notWritten`), retries, toast after 3; sync writes `arrived: true`, so `Con.md` lands where the platform can hold it | test/unwritable.test.ts; notes.rs; app killed mid-batch stays open, see status |

| B710 | High | Signed out, a file changed by git or an agent was overwritten by the next key in the open tab, no version (H3-DESKTOP-6) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | No watch without an account, and `write_note` writes without looking | `writeOne` always through `across.writing`; workspace/outside.svelte.ts runs the watch with no engine, re-read on focus | Tests "a stale buffer...", "what the watch says...", "the watch of the spaces"; fixed, not run on Tauri |
| B711 | High | A CRLF note open in two windows: a throw left a window unable to hear or save, and all its typing was lost (H3-WINDOWS-1) | Fixed, in the hunt integration PR ([#1094](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/1094)) | `NoteDoc` held raw CRLF over an LF rope, `took` set `quiet` before a call that can throw | `unixLines` wherever words come in; `quietly` in try/finally; a failed merge gives the rope the words, a failed write retries | across.test.ts "a file with CRLF endings"; drive windows-across.py; not run on Tauri, CEF, WebView2 or WKWebView |

### Hunt status

Integration: `claude/hunt-round-1`; the final run is in `findings/hunt-final.md`.
The reviews found many bugs in the fixes, folded above, and older ones with their own rows: B695, B698 to B702, B706, B716 to B724, B739, B740, B747, B757. B741 has no severity in its source. Commits not given for B650.

| High | 57 | 57 | 0 |
| Medium | 106 | 106 | 0 |

No Critical, High or Medium is wholly open. Left in part or as a limit: a typed reply with no window on macOS (B683); plain `<a href=/delete-account>` and a listener on a link's ancestor stay free, and a button that says nothing goes (B713, B717); a secret field shown and gone inside 70 ms between two scans (B702); an app killed in a pull batch can double those notes, up to `PULL_BATCH` (B705); a kill after the crate's write and before the session's persist writes old words back (B737); a fold's first stall in WebKit, cause not found (hunt-known 8); wayland deb's "a click on another tab" until `f645b7fb6` reaches this branch (hunt4-integ).
Needs a decision: H1-TAURI2-17, H1-TAURI2-18, H4-SYNC-L3 (B770, B780); `write_note`'s whole-text replace (B773).
Not run on platform or at all: B607, B620 to B622, B665, B666, B683 to B685; the windows rows of rounds 3 and 4 ran on the web build only.

## Search, links and tags

Started 2026-11-15 on `claude/project-thread-zpbwee`, from `unstable`. Scope: search (the
web and phone matcher in `packages/scan` and the desktop matcher in `src-tauri`), indexing,
wiki links and backlinks, rename-updates-links, tags and front matter properties. Three
read-only audits ran first (search and tags S1-S16, links L1-L12, the native index and its
parity with the web N1-N16), each finding traced to an input. Each fix starts from a
failing regression test. Four native search commits from the desktop backend thread (DB19,
DB20 and the pattern budget) are cherry-picked here too, so the two branches merge cleanly.

Merged with `unstable` on 2026-11-17, after #2139, #2141, #2144, #2145, #2148 and #2150 had
fixed some of the same things a second way. One design was kept per overlap: the block
spans, block names, export links in front matter, completion inside a written link and
the rename walk are this section's; the CLI thread's rule that a file named by its name
alone is the one beside the note first (as for notes) now holds for pictures and files
too, and its position tracking while a block is named, its picker alias and its cached
file lookup were kept. `links.fileFor` takes the link kind, wikilink by default.

| # | Severity | Bug | Status | Root cause | Fix | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| S1 | High | Renaming or moving up a tag that holds `İ` ate the character after the tag | Fixed `9041a0a5` | The span to replace was measured on the folded text, and `İ` folds to two characters | The span is taken from the name as written | `tags.test.ts` "as long as the name is written, even where folding makes it longer" (failed before) |
| S2 | High | Regex replace split an emoji into lone surrogates | Fixed `e4d13aec` | Patterns compiled without the `u` flag | Patterns compile with `u`, and fall back to the looser reading only when `u` refuses them | `match.test.ts` "take a character outside the basic plane whole", "still read the escapes and braces a looser reading allows" (failed before) |
| S3 | High | The search panel threw when a PDF matched on two or more pages | Fixed `36b0853f` | Rows were keyed by path, so pages of one paper shared a key | `hitKey` adds the page | `SearchPanel` test "shows a row for each page" (failed before) |
| S4 | Medium | A wikilink, markdown link or task box in a query was read as a front matter property | Fixed `94f5f5e5` | `[x]` and `[[a]]` matched the property bracket syntax | Brackets that are a link or a task box are words | query test "is an ordinary word when the brackets are a link or a task box" (failed before) |
| S5 | Medium | Ticking a task or replacing from results that went stale acted on whatever was at that line now | Fixed `9704e4f3` | Results carried only a line number | Results carry the task hash or line text; the act finds that line or does nothing | 3 tests in search actions (failed before) |
| S6 | Medium | Removing a tag from every note left its quotes behind in front matter | Fixed `57670160` | Only the tag text was cut | The quotes go with it | "and the quotes a tag was written in" (failed before) |
| S7 | Medium | `section:` ended at a `#` comment line inside a code fence | Fixed `61794042` (web), `f81c9c51` (desktop) | Heading lines were read without knowing about fences | Fenced lines are skipped | `match.test.ts`, `a_section_does_not_end_at_a_comment_inside_a_code_fence` (failed before) |
| S8 | Medium | Desktop silently found nothing for patterns the web takes: look-arounds, backreferences, named groups, `\x41`, `A`, `\u{…}`, `\cJ`, lone braces like `{{\w+}}` | Fixed `5ce473b2` | The desktop regex engine refused them | The engine runs look-aheads and look-behinds as atomic sub-runs, backreferences by number or name, code escapes, and reads a brace that counts nothing as a brace | 4 new `regex.rs` tests (failed before); see the differential note below |
| S9 | Low | `line:/done$/` missed in CRLF notes | Fixed `d7c7b8d8`, `f81c9c51` | The line end included `\r` | A line ends before its `\r` | tests on both sides (failed before) |
| S10 | Low | Capital sigma folded differently in the web and desktop search | Fixed `dba193b2` (web) | `toLowerCase` is context-aware for final sigma | Sigma folds the same wherever it sits | "folds a capital sigma the same wherever it sits in a word" (failed before) |
| S11 | Low | Desktop lowercased a property query value, breaking date and time comparison | Fixed `36c0eef9` | The asked value was lowercased before `a_date` | Only `has` comparisons fold | `matcher.rs` test (failed before) |
| S12 | Low | `task:` counted a checkbox inside a code fence | Fixed `69e8d6a5`, `f81c9c51` | Task lines ignored fences | Fenced lines are skipped | tests on both sides (failed before) |
| S13 | Low | Searching from a selection treated words like `OR` or `tag:` as operators | Fixed `8d5b39d7` | The selection went in as a raw query | `asWritten` quotes it | 2 query tests (failed before) |
| S14 | Low | A task box regex took several characters or a `]`, so emoji boxes broke | Fixed `cd82b008` | The box pattern was too loose | One character, astral included, never `]` | 2 tests (failed before) |
| S15 | Low | A tag with a doubled slash was not found as the tag tree shows it | Fixed `208371cf`, `ff8afa4f` | Search compared the raw tag | Both sides use `tagPath` | tests on both sides (failed before) |
| S16 | Low | Fuzzy highlights split an astral letter | Fixed `fa3f3201` | Marks were placed per UTF-16 unit | Marks cover the whole code point | "marks a letter outside the basic plane whole" (failed before) |
| L1 | High | A rename wrote the new name into a quoted YAML link without escaping, making the front matter invalid | Fixed `07c7abeb` | `retargetLinks` replaced text with no idea of the quoted scalar around it | Edits inside front matter swap the quote kind when safe, otherwise escape | 4 `link-rewrite.test.ts` tests (failed before) |
| L2 | Medium | Moving a note or renaming a folder left bare `[[Name]]` links that now reached a namesake | Fixed `ffb62a5b` | `stillReaches` asked only whether the old target still existed | Each link is resolved from where it will stand; one that would reach another note is rewritten to a path | 4 `link-index.test.ts` tests (failed before) |
| L3 | Medium | Links in a canvas's text cards were not rewritten on rename | Fixed `a6f0fbcc` | The rename only read canvases whose file cards named the note | Every canvas is read on a rename | "follows its note when the note is renamed, with no card naming the note" (failed before). Text-card links are still not backlinks, on purpose (an existing Rust test asserts it) |
| L4 | Medium | A rename while the space was still being indexed rewrote no links | Fixed `6ad9c089`, `73745855` | The current-space branch ran on a half-read index | It waits for the index when startup has reached it, otherwise rewrites from the disk | 2 `link-index.test.ts` tests (failed before); `workspace.test.ts` 364 passed |
| L5 | Medium | A link to an attachment no tab opens showed as missing, and Cmd-click made a note named after it | Fixed `a59ba20f` | `jumpFor` only resolved notes | It falls back to the file, and the workspace reveals it in the explorer | `workspace.test.ts`, `notes.test.ts` (failed before) |
| L6 | Medium | With two notes of one name, autocomplete wrote a link that opened the wrong one | Fixed `beb61515` | The plain name was always written | The shortest path suffix that resolves to the picked note is written | `complete.test.ts` (failed before) |
| L7 | Medium | Picking a completion inside an existing aliased or heading link corrupted it | Fixed `beb61515` | The rest of the old link was left in place | The pick replaces the rest of the name and keeps `#heading` and `|alias` | 4 `complete.test.ts` tests (failed before) |
| L8 | Low | Mentions counted a name inside a link to another note or inside inline code | Fixed `270d4a32` | `standsAlone` read the raw line | Links and code spans are blanked first | "a name only inside a link to another note or inside code is no mention" (failed before) |
| L12 | Low | Front matter comments were offered as headings after `[[Note#` | Fixed `df5b312e` (web), `945617c5` (desktop) | Headings were read from front matter lines | Front matter is skipped | tests on both sides (failed before) |
| QB | Medium | A task ticked in a query block after the note changed ticked the wrong task | Fixed `33418c8d` | The box carried only a line | Each box carries the task's hash | query-block test (failed before) |
| N1 | Medium | A desktop flow-list alias with a quoted comma split into broken pieces | Fixed `36c0eef9` | `split(',')` ignored quotes | A quote-aware split | `a_flow_list_member_keeps_a_comma_inside_its_quotes` (failed before) |
| N2 | Medium | A file renamed across the `.md` extension left the link index stale | Fixed `73745855` | `follow` treated it as a plain move | A move that changes what the path is held as goes out and comes back | "becomes a note when it takes the extension and stops being one when it drops it" (failed before) |
| N3-N10 | Low | Desktop parity: closing `#` of a heading word lost, YAML escapes not decoded, quoted keys ignored, trailing comments kept in values, `key:value` taken as a key, case-duplicate keys, an empty dash ending a list, `[[#^]]` a link | Fixed `36c0eef9`, `945617c5` | The desktop front matter and link readers were simpler than the web's | `key_of`, `key_line`, `unquoted`, `members`, `without_closing_hashes` and the empty-block check read as the web does | one `front_matter.rs` or `links.rs` test each (failed before) |
| N11-N13 | Low | Web parity: a lone `#` in a CRLF note or `#` before a no-break space made a heading, and a fence closed on mixed marks | Fixed `df5b312e` | `\s` took `\r` and ` `; the close test took any run of marks | Heading space is `[ \t]`; a close is one mark repeated | `links.test.ts`, `fences.test.ts` (failed before) |
| N14 | Low | Desktop linked canvas cards with no id or an empty file, and one ill-typed field dropped every link | Fixed `2fd81796` | The whole canvas was one serde struct | Cards are read one by one, needing an id and a file | `a_card_without_its_own_name_or_a_file_links_nowhere_and_spoils_no_other_card` (failed before) |
| N15 | Low | A desktop `.url` shortcut offered any address, `file:` and `javascript:` too | Fixed `945617c5` | No section or web-address check | Only `[InternetShortcut]`, only web addresses | `a_shortcut_to_an_address_that_is_not_a_web_page_points_nowhere` (failed before) |

| R1 | Medium | A rename while the space was being read could leave every link at the old name, when the rows were read after the rename on disk | Fixed `a0a15f0b` | `retarget` waited for the scan and then trusted rows that already held the new path | When the rows hold the new path and not the old one, the links are rewritten from the disk, mapped back | 2 `link-index.test.ts` tests (failed before) |
| R2 | Medium | Picking an unnamed block above the caret in `[[#^` wrote the link in the wrong place | Fixed `4ff16661` | Naming the block edits the note above the link, and the old positions were reused | The positions follow the caret, and nothing is written if the typed link is gone | `complete.test.ts` "a block named above the link still gets its link where the link was typed" (failed before) |
| R3 | Medium | A link to a `.txt` file showed it in the file manager instead of opening it | Fixed `68b7236a` | `opensInNoTab` took the default opener as "no tab", but plain text opens as a note | Plain text counts as a note, as the explorer reads it | `workspace.test.ts` (failed before) |
| R4 | Medium | Web property search disagreed with desktop: block lists, quotes, comments, quoted keys | Fixed `933a579a` (cherry-picked from the CLI thread) | The web read raw text after the first colon | It reads values with the front matter reader | `match.test.ts`; repro `[authors:herbert]` and `[status:old]` now agree |
| R5 | Medium | The web read a YAML comment after front matter tags as tags, and a tag removal could rewrite the comment | Fixed `e2331175` (cherry-picked from the CLI thread) | The single-line and dash-item paths kept the comment | Comments are dropped first | `tags.test.ts`; `tags: a # old` gives `[a]` |
| R6 | Medium | A checkbox found inside a code fence could be ticked from search results | Fixed `21d6115e` | The tick read only the one line | `toggleTaskAt` refuses a line inside a fence | `note-text.test.ts` "a box inside a code fence is an example, never ticked" (failed before) |
| R7 | Medium | An unbounded look-behind on desktop ran out of budget on ordinary notes and silently dropped hits (82 of 400) | Fixed `260e5102` | Look-behinds tried every earlier start, O(n) per position | Look-behinds run backward from where they stand, as JavaScript does, which also gives its captures | `a_look_behind_of_any_length_reads_back_only_as_far_as_it_needs` (failed before); see the differential note below |
| R8 | Low | A rename turned apostrophes in a plain front matter value into double quotes | Fixed `a47b7f8c` | Any `'` on the line opened a quoted scalar | A quote opens a scalar only where YAML lets one start | `link-rewrite.test.ts` (failed before) |
| R9 | Low | Desktop kept a byte order mark in a found line, so replace-all skipped that line | Fixed `51915f67` | Rust `trim` keeps U+FEFF; JavaScript's strips it | The line is trimmed with the same set as JavaScript `\s` | `a_line_is_shown_without_the_marks_the_web_trims_off_it` (failed before) |

| R10 | Medium | A markdown link as the Links setting writes it ("shortest" `[Plan](Plan.md)` or "absolute" `[Plan](ideas/Plan.md)`) was unresolved: no backlink, broken by a rename, and a click made a duplicate note | Fixed `b7a77052` | Markdown targets were only read relative to the linking note | When the relative reading finds nothing and the target is not written `./`, `../` or `/`, it is read from the top of the space, then by name | `wikilink.test.ts`, `link-index.test.ts` (failed before) |
| R11 | Medium | A link to a `.base` database (and to a chat, terminal or app file) opened its raw text as a note | Fixed `b25c1a44` | `followLink` and `followAside` only knew pdf, pages and canvas | Kinds with their own tab go through `openEntry` | `workspace.test.ts` "opens a linked database as a database, never as its text" (failed before) |
| R12 | Medium | On the web a front matter block list in a CRLF note was not read, so property search and desktop replace-all with a property filter skipped the note | Fixed `c817b3ce` | The list item pattern refused a line ending in `\r` | It takes a trailing `\r` | `front-matter.test.ts`, `match.test.ts` (failed before) |
| R13 | Low | Desktop counted an empty round of `?` or `{n,m}` past its least, unlike JavaScript | Fixed `404bcc1a` | Only `*` and `+` rounds checked for progress | Optional rounds check it too | `a_counted_round_past_the_least_that_takes_nothing_does_not_count` (failed before); see the differential note below |
| R14 | Low | A backreference over a long group could keep one desktop search busy for seconds | Fixed `404bcc1a` | A backreference cost one budget step however much it read | It spends one step per byte it reads | `calling_a_long_group_back_spends_the_budget_it_reads` (140 s before, instant after) |

| R15 | Medium | Renaming a picture or other attachment broke markdown links that reached it by name or from the top | Fixed `81dd8765` | The rename's file lookup had none of R10's fallback | It reads the target from the top, then by name, as the editor does | `link-index.test.ts` "is followed by a rename however the link was written" (failed before) |
| R16 | Medium | Desktop listed hits for nested-repeat patterns such as `/(\d+\.?)+/` that the web refuses as too costly, then replace-all silently replaced nothing | Fixed `774b4d45`, `f6b6b768` | The web refuses such shapes up front; desktop ran them; replace-all re-matches with the web matcher | The page marks a refused pattern before it goes to desktop, and desktop refuses it too | `space-locked.test.ts`, `a_pattern_the_page_refused_finds_nothing` (failed before) |
| R17 | Medium | Desktop missed a front matter list whose key line ends in a comment (`aliases: # names` then dashes), and a multi-line flow list or quoted value whose first line ends in a space | Fixed `7459b058` | The comment was not stripped before the "empty value" check, and the value's offset was taken from a trimmed length | Strip the comment first; take the offset from the value's place in the line | `a_list_reads_past_a_comment_on_its_key_and_a_space_at_the_end_of_its_first_line` (failed before) |
| R18 | Medium | A rename that had to escape a quote in front matter (`up: '[[Plan]] "x"'` renamed to `Bob's plan` gives `[[Bob''s plan]]`) left a link that neither the web nor desktop could read, so backlinks lost it | Fixed `afb8f75e` | Both link readers took front matter links from the raw text without undoing YAML quote escapes | Front matter lines are read with `''`, `\"`, `\\` and `\/` undone inside a quoted value (web keeps the raw offsets for rewrites) | `links.test.ts` "a link in a quoted front matter value is read the way the value means it", `link-rewrite.test.ts` round trip, Rust `a_link_in_a_quoted_front_matter_value_is_read_the_way_the_value_means_it` (all failed before); 160,000 random lines read the same on both sides |
| R19 | Medium | Renaming or moving a note next to a bare link (`a/Draft.md` to `a/Plan.md` beside `a/n.md` = `[[Plan]]`) silently took that link over from its namesake `Plan.md` elsewhere | Fixed `8654e907` | The rewrite skipped every link whose target was not moving, and never checked whether the arriving note now caught it | Links named like an arriving note are checked against the space after the move and rewritten to a path that still reaches their note (`[[/Plan]]`, `../Plan.md`) | `link-index.test.ts` "a note renamed beside a link to its new namesake ..." and "a note moved beside ..." (failed before) |
| R20 | Medium | Desktop regex search dropped hits on notes with long lines (`/.*TODO/` found 0 of 1 on four 2000-character lines, 36 of 100 on 300-character lines) while the web found them, and replace-all then replaced only part | Fixed `ca067896` | A pattern led by an unbounded run costs the square of the line length per line, and the per-note step budget ran out | When an attempt led by single letters and an unbounded run fails, desktop skips every later start that run already covered, so such patterns cost about the line length | `a_pattern_led_by_a_long_run_finds_every_hit_on_long_lines` (failed before), `skipping_past_a_leading_run_finds_what_trying_every_start_finds`; about 62,000 random led patterns gave the same hits and groups with and without the skip |
| R21 | Medium | A markdown link by name (`[x](Plan.md)` in `Projects/n.md`) reached a deeper `Archive/Projects/Plan.md` before the top `Plan.md`, and link completion wrote such links | Fixed `ae34cff5` | `resolveRelative` suffix-matched the folded relative path anywhere in the space before reading the target from the top | That step is gone: the path as written, then from the top, then by name | `wikilink.test.ts` "a markdown target by name alone reaches the note at the top ..." (failed before) |
| R22 | Medium | A move that made another namesake nearer left a markdown link by name (`[x](Road.md)`) reaching the wrong note, while the wikilink beside it was fixed | Fixed `f1597d5f` | Markdown links were judged by a string test that ignored which namesake the by-name reading picks | When only the target moves, the link is resolved against the space after the move, as wikilinks are | `link-index.test.ts` "a markdown link by name keeps reaching its note ..." and "... follows its note past a namesake that is now nearer" (failed before) |
| R23 | Medium | Renaming a note to an alias another note answers to (`Draft` to `Bob` while `People/Robert` has `aliases: [Bob]`), or moving one of two notes sharing an alias, took the alias links over | Fixed `4b155081` | Alias links were skipped by the rewrite, and the moving notes' aliases were never checked | Alias links named like an arriving note or a moving alias are resolved after the move; if they would land elsewhere they become `[[Robert\|Bob]]` (keeping the shown words, `\|` in a table) | `link-index.test.ts` "a note renamed to an alias ..." and "a note moved away from an alias link ..." (failed before) |
| R24 | Medium | Backlinks and outgoing links missed links through an alias holding a slash (`[[TCP/IP]]`), though the editor resolved them | Fixed `c9f10e84` | The index looked notes up by the last path segment, while aliases were keyed whole | Lookups also try the whole target | `link-index.test.ts` "an alias that holds a slash" (failed before) |
| R25 | Medium | Clicking an unresolved markdown link `./New.md` in `B/x.md` made `New.md` at the top; `../Up.md` made nothing | Fixed `099b5132` | The jump carried no link kind, so making the note read every target as a wikilink path from the top | The jump carries its kind; a markdown target is read from the note's folder | `workspace.test.ts` "a markdown link to no note makes it in the folder its path reads from" (failed before) |
| R26 | Medium | Taking away a tag left the second of two side by side (`#work #work/sub`, `tags: [work, work/sub]`), and `tags: [work , home]` became invalid YAML | Fixed `feffc753` | Two cuts claimed the same separator and the second was dropped; a space before a comma stopped the comma being taken | Overlapping cuts merge; the comma after spaces goes with the tag | `tag-edits.test.ts` "two tags taken away side by side" (failed before) |
| R27 | Medium | Desktop still dropped hits for a leading run inside a group or an alternation (`(.*)TODO`, `.*TODO\|FIXME`: 0 of 1 on 500-character paragraphs) | Fixed `7255ef7a` | The R20 skip only took a bare leading run | The skip looks through groups when nothing calls a group back, and through each branch of a top-level alternation, taking the nearest start any branch allows | `a_pattern_led_by_a_long_run_finds_every_hit_on_long_lines` (new cases failed before); about 78,000 more random led patterns gave the same hits and groups with and without the skip |
| R28 | Medium | A folded pattern such as `/(a\|A)*b/i` hung the web search worker (6.8 s on 26 letters) and went to desktop unrefused | Fixed `073609ef` | The cost judge was asked without the `i` flag | The web matcher and the desktop hand-off pass the folding flag | `match.test.ts` "is judged with the case it was asked to ignore", `space-locked.test.ts` (both failed before) |
| R29 | Medium | A pattern such as `/(\p{L}+)+;/` or `/(\u{61}+)+;/` hung the web search worker (41.8 s on one sentence) and could freeze desktop's draft search | Fixed `0f0f408e` | The cost judge read the pattern without the `u` flag it then compiles with, so `\p{…}` and `\u{…}` were not seen as one letter | Search patterns are judged in the mode they compile in | `match.test.ts` "is judged in the Unicode mode it runs in", `space-locked.test.ts` (both failed before) |
| R30 | Low, fixed | Desktop `.` took a carriage return, so `/TODO./` hit CRLF lines the web does not, and replace-all changed nothing there | Fixed `b33d48cd` | Desktop's dot stopped only at `\n` | It stops at `\n`, `\r`, U+2028 and U+2029, as JavaScript does | `a_dot_takes_anything_but_a_newline` (failed before) |
| R31 | Low, fixed | Removing `#home` from `#home,#plan` turned `#plan` into a new tag | Fixed `b32bd8e7` | The cut took the comma that kept `#plan` from being a tag | A comma right before another `#` stays | `tag-edits.test.ts` "a comma right before another mark stays" (failed before) |

(two, failed before) |
| R33 | Medium | Moving one of two notes sharing an alias nearer to a link took `[[Bob]]` over | Fixed `172e5173` | The alias branch returned early unless a file name arrived, ignoring moving aliases | A moving alias also triggers the check; `[[Bob\|]]` and a row without a leading pipe get the shown words right | `link-index.test.ts` "another note with the alias moving nearer ..." (failed before) |

| R39 | Medium | With many notes sharing a name (`Export/Page i/index.md`), a folder rename took 22 s at 4,000 notes, and the backlinks panel took 4.4 s after every save | Fixed `544285ad` | Each resolution folded and sorted every same-named note, and the cache is cleared on each save | Notes are looked up by every end of their path and by alias, and the nearest pick is kept per list | `link-index.perf.effect.test.ts`: at 1,000 notes the rename does 32,001 name folds (5,030,001 before) and the backlinks 11,995 (3,008,995 before); 60,000 random cases resolve the same as the old resolver |
| R40 | Medium | Patterns that passed the cost judge hung the web search worker and the desktop draft search for seconds to minutes on ordinary notes (`/(.*) (.*) (.*)QQQ/` 12 s, `/.* .* .* .*QQQ/` 222 s on 20 KB of this file) | Fixed `0e0636a4` | The judge summed unbounded runs and refused only nested ones, so runs in a row that trade letters (cost about the length to the power of the runs) always passed | The judge now also weighs each run by how far it can reach (a word, a line, the note) and whether what follows can take its letters, and refuses a pattern over the limit | `pattern-cost.test.ts` (the auditor's patterns, failed before); a sweep of 3,000 random patterns on three 20 KB notes leaves no allowed pattern over 250 ms |

| R43 | Low, fixed | `[n:null]` missed `n: null` and `n: ~` | Fixed `455ae28a` | Only an empty value counted as null | `null`, `Null`, `NULL` and `~` count as null on both sides | `match.test.ts` (failed before), `a_key_yaml_reads_as_null_holds_nothing` |
| R44 | Medium | After R40 the judge refused everyday patterns (`^\s*(\w+)\s*:\s*(.*)$`, `"[^"]*"`, `\s+-\s+`, the wikilink pattern) and let `[\s\S]*eX` or `(.*) (.*)\d+` through, which take seconds on a 200 KB note | Fixed `a08f9256` | Each run was weighed alone, so a run that the next letters cannot take cost as much as one that trades letters with them, and a run that ends a pattern counted for nothing | The judge walks the pattern from the end and knows which letters can come next: a run whose letters the next part cannot take adds its own reach once, a run that trades letters multiplies, and a trailing run that must take a letter still counts | `pattern-cost.test.ts` (refused and may-run lists, failed before); 195 everyday patterns all run except six note-wide `[^x]+x` forms with nothing in front; sweeps on a 2,000-letter line and three 20 KB notes leave no allowed pattern over 43 ms |
| R45 | Low, fixed | Taking `#work` out of `Tags: #work, #home` left two spaces | Fixed `fd341437` | The R42 rule that keeps one space before a comment also ran in the body, where `#` starts a tag | The rule runs only inside front matter | `tags.test.ts` "and in the body a cut before the next tag leaves one space" (failed before) |
