| AI-56 | Low | A move's time was taken before its print was worked out, so another window's later move could land first and cursors skip this one (eighth re-audit) | Fixed `1d3cbe2f` | `Date.now()` ran before the await | The time is taken once the print is known | Same test; reasoning |
| AI-57 | Medium | Found while testing AI-55: since `5d3b66c6` read the ledger again before each move, a window whose web storage cannot be written forgot its earlier moves, so chats and agent names stopped following them | Fixed `1d3cbe2f` | Reading again replaced the moves held in memory with the empty stored list | Reading again keeps what was held and not read back | `moves.test.ts` "moves kept where web storage cannot be written are still known to the window" (failed before) |
| AI-58 | Low (re-audit said Medium) | Known limit, not fixed: a move is known by the account that stayed, so a second local account moved off the same id because of the same remote account (the first moved account removed, the remote one removed and added again on another device, a new local account added on that id before that syncs) reads as a copy of the first move (ninth re-audit) | Open | Telling the moved account apart needs its key, and that is exactly what a second window may read after the first one rewrote it (AI-55); knowing the move by both would bring AI-55 back, which is far likelier | Would need account ids unique across devices, so the vault never moves an account; rated Low because it takes four separate steps on two devices in that order | None |
| AI-59 | Low | Known limit, not fixed: when the vault's write back fails after a merge took the accounts, the old base stays, so a later key change on the account that stayed can read as a clash and move it again (ninth re-audit) | Open | The base only moves on when the write back succeeds | Would need the base kept once the accounts are taken | None |

## Tabs, browser and PDF

Burn-down of 2026-11-15 on `claude/project-thread-w9q8z3`, from `unstable`. Area: tabs and the tab overview, the new-tab page, the built-in web browser (CEF), downloads, the PDF and pages viewers and the terminal. Method as above: three read-only audit passes, then for each bug a failing regression test, root cause, fix, and the area's tests, eslint and prettier again. One commit per bug.

| # | Severity | Bug | Status | Root cause | Fix | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TB1 | High | In the browser build, a rename or move of a PDF or picture onto one of the same name, or of a folder onto one holding only pictures, overwrote it (and its highlights) | Fixed `b3e99118` | `renameNote` checked only the `files` store; binaries live in `assets` | `renameNote` and `copyPath` refuse when `holds(target)`, which also looks in `assets` | `commands.test.ts` 3 tests (failed before); web-backend, workspace, pdf 1515 passed |
| TB2 | High | An agent's command ran without asking on fish (`\x2d` escapes), PowerShell (curly quotes, `a,--opt`), cmd (`^`, single quotes) and nushell (`r#'…'#`): e.g. `git fetch \x2d\x2dupload-pack=… .` | Fixed `551e1c3f` | The free-run check split words and denied metacharacters the POSIX way only | `onTheList` takes only plain ASCII with no backslash, no comma before a dash, and on Windows no single-quoted span holding ` -` | `shell-text.test.ts` 13 new (10 failed before); terminal 551 passed |
| TB3 | High | Any page or ad frame could open unlimited front tabs with no press by dispatching `orden-open-tab` | Fixed `06ddd9e6` | The isolated world opened on a DOM event the page can dispatch itself | It keeps its own trusted presses and opens once per press with user activation; anything else goes to the popup-blocked bar | `page-keys.test.ts` 2 tests (failed before); cargo `web_opens` tests |
| TB4 | Medium | An address entered while a web tab's page was still being built was dropped and the bar snapped back | Fixed `4c72b77d` | `go()` during `opening` only set `page.url`, which engine events overwrite; a successful build never navigated | `Pages` remembers the address and goes to it once the page arrives (build and adopt) | `pages.test.ts` 2 tests (1 failed before); web-tab 640 passed |
| TB5 | Medium | A tab dropped in the overview before another landed one slot right for each essential before it | Fixed `23ccfeb3` | `drop()` indexed with `heldIn` (with essentials); `moveTab` reads a strip index | `stripPlace()` computes the index among the strip's tabs | `free.test.ts` 2 tests; overview 94 passed |
| TB6 | Medium | A web shortcut file was left on a page the tab had already left (A, B, back to A within the save delay, e.g. an SSO bounce) | Fixed `7cc80eea` | The nothing-changed check compared with the file and left B's pending write | The pending write is cancelled when the page is back to what the file says | `keep.test.ts` 1 test (failed before) |
| TB7 | Medium | After the page moved while the address field had focus, the bar, site permissions, zoom, mute and the shortcut file stayed on the old page | Fixed `bece98ee` | Engine events skip `page.url` while typing and nothing caught up on blur | `Pages.typing()` takes `page.at` when typing ends, unless an address was gone to | `pages.test.ts` 2 tests; web-tab 645 passed |
| TB8 | Medium | Dragging on a page did nothing in every PDF tab and read-only pages note: no pan, no pinch, no long-press word pick | Fixed `2e498c6f` | `Pages.svelte` returned on `!writable` before the select tool's fall-through to a pan | `pans(tool, onPage, writable)`: a read-only page always pans | `starts.test.ts` 3 tests; pages 142 passed |
| TB9 | Medium | Exporting or printing a shared PDF-backed pages note as PDF gave blank sheets with only the ink | Fixed `c5caf600` | `pagesPdf` read the paper from disk paths only and ignored `shared` and the account copy | `paperBytesOf(at)` reads the places, then the account copy | `out.paper.test.ts` 1 test (failed before); pages, pdf, export 807 passed |
| TB10 | Medium | The pages navigator drew ink-only thumbnails (retrying an open on every change) for a shared PDF-backed note | Fixed `4acbe28e` | Its `PaperAt` lacked `shared`, unlike every other caller | `PagesStore.paperAt(file, root)` carries the note's shared id | `store.test.ts` 1 test; pages 144 passed |
| TB11 | Medium | Pass (autofill, marks, save offer) stopped working on a page reached by a cross-site navigation | Fixed `4b014e77` | The context-to-frame cache is keyed by a CDP context id, which a new renderer process reuses; a stale hit dropped every message | `placed_by`: a cached place no longer where it was is forgotten and the frames learnt again | `web_pass` cargo tests, 3 new |
| TB12 | Low | Close all tabs was greyed out and its shortcut did nothing when no strip tab was in front | Fixed `c61d9f07` | Both anchored on the front tab | `closeAllIn(paneId)` / `closesAllIn` | `workspace.test.ts`, `strip-menu.test.ts` |
| TB13 | Low | Ctrl+Tab did nothing while an essential was in front | Fixed `55b281ea` | Only a null front tab fell back to the strip | An id not in the strip falls back too | tab-cycle `contract.test.ts` (failed before) |
| TB14 | Low | Duplicating an essential put the copy at the start of the strip | Fixed `05c574ba` | `indexOf` -1 + 1 = 0 | Append when the tab is not in the strip | `workspace.test.ts` (failed before) |
| TB15 | Low | A closed split pane's tabs reopened at the dead pane's positions inside the focused pane | Fixed `763644fa` | `closed.at` of the dead pane applied as is | Offset after the tabs the pane already had | `workspace.test.ts` (failed before) |
| TB16 | Low | A restart showed the first strip tab, not the last used, when a private or essential tab was in front | Fixed `2ac1b8d6` | `layout()` wrote `active` as `max(0, -1)` | Writes -1, so `built()` takes the recent order | `workspace.test.ts` (failed before); 1432 passed |
| TB17 | Low | The site bubble kept the previous site's certificate when the new read failed | Fixed `1df07523` | The effect only cleared on a non-https address | Cleared before every read | svelte-check, eslint (no harness changes WebSite's props) |
| TB18 | Low | A page leaving full screen within one IPC round trip left the window full screen and its page invisible | Fixed `d0ceeee7` | `take()` awaited before setting the holder | take and give run in turn | `filling.test.ts` (failed before) |
| TB19 | Low | Delete browsing data could leave a cleared site in the site list when an answer was in flight | Fixed `1ce1b99c` | In-flight answers wrote their rows back | A clearing count drops older answers | `site-icons.test.ts` 2 tests (failed before) |
| TB20 | Low | A progress report after a cancel brought the download back as going, with a dead cancel button | Fixed `24d0d40d` | A cancelled id left the list, so a late report looked new | Stopped ids are remembered | `downloads.test.ts` (failed before) |
| TB21 | Low | A host list refresh answered after a save took the save back, and the next save wrote the stale list | Fixed `1e78f3c8` | No ordering between `refresh` and `keep` | A change count | `remote/kept.test.ts` (failed before) |
| TB22 | Low | An ended online session came back from an earlier refresh | Fixed `a6203e24` | No last-request-wins guard | A request count | `online/sessions.test.ts` (failed before) |
| TB23 | Low | A refused host address put back only the hostname; unpinning left `pinned: false` | Fixed `9d8cbc64` | Revert used `detail ?? hostname`; pin spread `about` by hand | Revert to the shown text; `withAbout` | eslint, svelte-check |
| TB24 | Low | `a_private_tab_s_popup_is_private` passed vacuously | Fixed `3088c490` | `fn popup(` is gone; the split matched the test's own literal | Removed; private popups ride CEF's opener context | cargo `web_tabs` |
| TB25 | Low | Dropping a link whose words hold `&#x110000;` threw and made no web note | Fixed `36a8eaa2` | `String.fromCodePoint` throws past U+10FFFF | Left as written | `dropped-site.test.ts` (failed before) |
| TB26 | Low | The Rust lib tests did not compile on `unstable`: `page_scale.rs` read `src/lib/modes.svelte.ts`, moved by #2101 | Fixed `48bc4e2c` | Stale `include_str!` path | New path | cargo test compiles |
| TB27 | High | Re-audit: an agent's command still ran without asking on cmd and PowerShell: `^` escapes, `%VAR:a=b%` substitution and `%VAR:~n,m%` slices write a dash, and a mix of `'` and `"` quotes ends a span where POSIX does not (`git fetch . 'x"'" --upload-pack=calc"`) | Fixed `fe5767d5` | TB2's plain-text check allowed `^`, `%` and both quotes everywhere, and refused accented letters | On Windows, `readAlikeEverywhere` refuses `^`, any `%…%` span, a doubled quote and a line holding both quote kinds; letters with accents go everywhere, `HEAD^` on unix | `shell-text.test.ts` 8 cases (failed before); terminal agent 296 passed |
| TB28 | Medium | Re-audit: a link with `target=_blank rel=noopener` pressed inside an iframe was blocked as a popup | Fixed `0dc30af1` | TB3 counted only presses seen by the top frame's isolated world | A press within 5 s counts once; with none seen, live user activation counts once per 5 s | `page-keys.test.ts` activation-only case (failed before); cargo `web_opens` 23 passed |
| TB29 | Medium | Re-audit: Share… in a web tab's menu was always enabled and did nothing | Fixed `a0d4228b` | `WebTab.svelte` passed no `share` action, and the menu row did not know | `share` is offered only for a tab with a shareable file; the row is greyed out without it | `menu.test.ts` 1 test (failed before) |
| TB30 | Low | Re-audit: one press opened up to four tabs (mousedown, pointerup, mouseup and click each counted) | Fixed `0dc30af1` | Every press-like event renewed the press | Only trusted keydown and pointerdown start a press, and a press is spent once | `page-keys.test.ts` (failed before) |
| TB31 | Low | Re-audit: the first screen chose a pane's tab at index -1 after a restart with a private tab in front | Fixed `1a2a60a0` | `active` can be -1 since TB16 | `-1` gives no first tab | `first-screen.test.ts` (failed before) |
| TB32 | Low | Re-audit: a failed host save left the list on the unsaved edit | Fixed `9fcc6fc4` | The catch path did not read back the saved list | It waits for any refresh and reads the list again | `remote/kept.test.ts` (failed before) |

Final check: the whole desktop vitest suite (17,664 passed; the only failures were the re-audit tests written before their fixes and the startup byte budget, raised by this branch to 2,001,200, all passing on rerun), svelte-check 0 errors, cargo `web_opens` 23 passed. The final re-audit found the rows TB27 to TB32, and a review of their fixes found nothing new above Low; that is what was audited, not a claim that the area has no bugs.

Open, accepted as insignificant Lows: an essential closed with its pane reopens as a strip tab at the start; a page can put an address of its choosing in its own popup-blocked bar; the certificate read made apart from the engine says secure without checking; `lend` can hand back a PDF rewritten within 10 s; session cookies of a closing window are written one call each within a fixed 2 s; Pass's frame-to-session map grows for a tab's life; `own-data` choose writes words read before its confirm; Reload on a tab whose page is unloaded does nothing until it is shown; a private window's downloads show in a normal window's bubble; the search engine label reads Custom for a known engine set by URL; `Tabs.svelte` still holds a comment from before.

## Frontend

Burn-down of 2026-11-15 from `unstable` at `7282b83a`, frontend only (editor, tabs, sidebar,
bases and boards, onboarding, settings UI, web and PWA client code). Tests on Node 24.

| # | Severity | Bug | Status | Root cause | Fix | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| F1 | Low | `vault/start.test.ts` "a hub open again reads the vault again" failed on a cold module cache (reported by the CLI/MCP thread) | Fixed | `startVault` imports the whole sync-client api lazily; the first transform of it took over the 1 s `vi.waitFor` budget, so `opened` was still empty | The test loads the sync-client api before starting the vault, so the lazy import resolves at once | Cold cache (vitest cache removed) failed 1/1 before, passed 3/3 after |
| F2 | Medium | Dragging an edge of a one-date timeline bar (a task with only a date, a scheduled date or a deadline) moved the whole item instead of stretching it | Fixed | `dragged` wrote the dragged edge to `bar.to`, which for a one-date bar is the same field as `bar.from`; only the start edge of a deadline-only task was special-cased | `stretched` turns an edge drag of a one-date task into a span: the date stays and a deadline is written at the far end (or a start before a deadline); only a bar drawn from a task's own dates (`ownDates`) is stretched that way; a bar from a date property the view names, with no end property, writes nothing on an edge drag (the re-audit caught a first version that wrote a deadline such a view never shows) | `timeline.test.ts` (11 tests, 7 failed before); views suite 143 passed |
| F3 | Low | The modes golden test compared against nothing: its snapshot stayed in `src/lib/__snapshots__` when the test moved to `modes/src`, so a local run wrote a fresh one and a CI run failed 32 tests | Fixed | The #2101 move took the test file but not its `__snapshots__` file | Snapshot moved beside the test | `CI=1` run: 32 failed without it, 33 passed with it; no orphaned `.snap` left in the repo |
| F4 | Medium | Board swimlanes came in the order the columns first met them, so with a value missing from the first column the lanes were out of order and "No value" sat in the middle (`Doing`: 1, 3, none; `Todo`: 2 gave lanes 1, 3, none, 2) | Fixed | Each column's sub-groups were sorted on their own and `laneKeys` took their union in insertion order | The sub-group order is one `orderKeys` in `answer`, which also orders every lane of the answer into `Answer.lanes`; `laneKeys` follows it for the lanes the shown columns hold | `answer.test.ts` "the lanes across every column…" (failed before), `list-items.test.ts` 2 lane tests; bases 1051 and views 132 passed; tsc, eslint, prettier clean |
| F5 | Medium | Any change in the filter builder rewrote a quoted value that reads like a number, truth or date as a bare one, so `note.zip == "007"` became `note.zip == 007` and the view lost its rows | Fixed | `unliteral` dropped the quotes and `literal` guessed the kind from the text alone; since B296 `"007"` and `7` are not equal | A row read from a quoted literal that `literal` would not write back the same way is kept `quoted`, and written back quoted; choosing another property reads the value afresh (`withProperty`). The re-audit caught a first version that also quoted numbers typed for any property holding text, which turned `note.ratings.contains(5)` on a list of numbers into `contains("5")`; that part is gone, so a number typed for a text property is still read as a number, as before | `filter-rows.test.ts` (5 tests: the quoted round trips failed before; a number in a list stays a number; a property change drops the quote); views suite 141 passed |
| F6 | Medium | In the first-launch setup, an import, a notes import, a sign-in code or a browser preset that finished after Back (or after "Use without an account") moved the setup on from whatever step was showing: back on the browser step it jumped to Import again, or skipped the next browser's import, or skipped Appearance | Fixed | Each step's async work ended in an unconditional `setup.next()` and never asked whether its step was still the one showing | `setup.stillHere()` hands work a check that is true only while the same visit of that step is showing; the import, notes, account and browser steps move on (and the notes step records its answer) only if it holds | `ImportStep.effect.test.ts` (failed before: `'import'` instead of `'browser'`), `setup.test.ts` "tells work started on a step…"; onboarding 47 passed |
| F7 | Medium | In the setup's AI step every wrong key typed for a keyed compatible provider (OpenRouter and the like) left one more broken account in Settings, AI; a wrong key typed for Anthropic, OpenAI or Gemini replaced the working key of an account already there, or was left behind on skip | Fixed | `connectCompatible` added an account on each try and only the keyless branch removed it on failure; `keyed` wrote the key before trying it and put nothing back when the try failed | `keyed` knows whether it made the account: on failure a made account is removed and its key forgotten, and a held account gets back the key it had | `connect.test.ts` (3 tests, all failed before); onboarding suite passed |
| F8 | Medium | "Close other tabs", "Close tabs to the right", "Close all", closing a pane or switching layout with exactly one unsaved draft among the tabs: after Save and picking a place only the draft closed, the rest stayed | Fixed | With one unsaved tab `mayLose` handed the save-place picker a callback that closed only that tab (`closeAsking(only.id)`) and answered `false`, so the batch was dropped | `mayLose` takes the whole close to run again once the draft is saved; `closeAsking`, `closeMany`, `closePane` and `useLayout` each pass their own | `workspace.test.ts` "Save on the one draft among several tabs closes all of them…" (failed before); workspace suite 986 passed |
| F9 | Medium | On Windows a folder selected together with something inside it was acted on twice: Delete trashed the folder and then failed on the file inside with a "not deleted" notice, a move failed the same way, and Copy or Duplicate made an extra top-level copy of the file | Fixed | `couldHold` listed a path's folders by splitting on `/` only, so with Windows paths no selected row was ever inside another and `outermost` kept both | The folders are found at every `/` or `\` | `selection.test.ts` "leaves out what a Windows folder takes along" (failed before); tree suite 242 passed, including the lookup cost test. Run on Linux with Windows paths, not on Windows |
| F10 | Medium | Paste from the menus did not do what Cmd/Ctrl+V does: Edit > Paste inside a code block turned copied code into a fence of its own (closing the user's fence) and tab separated text into a table; the right-click Paste left the caret before the pasted words, pasted only at the main cursor and took plain text only | Fixed | `pasteHere` had no code-block check, unlike `richPaste`; the editor menu had its own `paste` that dispatched a bare change with no selection | `pasteHere` inserts the text as it is when any cursor is in code, through the same `inCode` check `richPaste` uses; the editor menu's Paste runs `pasteHere` | `paste-here.test.ts` (3 tests, 2 failed before); editor 2073 passed, tsc clean; editor-menu and native-menu suites passed |
| F11 | Low | An on/off setting of a theme not worn (Glass "follow" and "tab", the wallpaper theme's "empty") read as off to agents though it starts on, so an agent turning it off wrote nothing and answered that it worked | Fixed | The switch branch of `themeField` read `get() === true` with no fallback to the setting's start value, unlike the range, colour and select branches | The switch falls back to `setting.initial` like the others | `theme-switches.test.ts` compares every declared field with its start value on a fresh profile (failed before on `themes.glass.follow`); settings and agent-host suites passed |
| F12 | Low | "Add" in a table or list grouped by a checkbox or a date made a note without the group's value, so it showed up in another group | Fixed | `addNote` copied the group key only when it was a string or a number | `groupProperties` also carries a truth and a date (as its plain value); the board's "New page" already did | `add.test.ts` (2 tests; the truth and date cases are what the old string-or-number check skipped); views suite passed |
| F13 | Low | On the todos board in manual order, dropping a card on the card just below it did nothing; moving a card down one place took a drop two cards further | Fixed | A drop on a card always meant "before it", so the card below was the place the card already had | The half of the card the drop lands on decides: the upper half is before it, the lower half after it (`dropAt`) | `tasks-views/test/drop-at.test.ts` (2 tests); tasks-views suite 9 passed, its contract test included; the board drop not run in a browser |
| F14 | Low | Going Back to a note that has gone from the disk left the tab's place in its trail one step ahead of the note it shows, so Forward was greyed out though there was a note ahead | Fixed | `walk` dropped the gone note from the trail and only clamped `at`, not moving it back past the entries removed before it | `at` moves back by the gone entries that were behind it | `workspace.test.ts` "and stays on the note it shows when the gone one was behind it…" (failed before); workspace suite 987 passed |
| F15 | Low | A copy or paste of a file that failed (no right to write there, disk full) left its placeholder row in the sidebar until the next reload | Fixed | `copyEntry` showed the new row before copying and returned on failure without taking it away | The row is taken out again when the copy fails | `copying.test.ts` "a copy that fails…" (failed before); workspace suite 988 passed |
| F16 | Low | A note cut or copied in the sidebar and then renamed, or whose folder was renamed or moved, before Paste was quietly not pasted | Fixed | The file clipboard held the paths as they were and did not follow file operations; Paste dropped paths no longer in the tree | The clipboard follows file operations like the selection does: moved paths are rewritten, removed ones let go | `list-landing.test.ts` (2 tests, both failed before); workspace and file-ops suites 1033 passed |
| F17 | Low | Opening Recently deleted could erase from the device trash a different note at the same place: a note deleted here after the account's deletion of a note of that name (made again and deleted before it synced, or differing only in case) was taken as the account's copy and purged | Fixed by the desktop thread in [#2139](https://github.com/lxorb/orden/pull/2139) (their DB22), not on this branch | `doublesOf` could only match on place and time: a device trash entry did not record whether sync or the reader put it there | `TrashEntry` records `arrived`, the sync paths pass it, and `doublesOf` takes only arrived entries | See #2139; reproduced here by the audit with `doublesOf` (account `plan.md` at 1000, device `Notes/Plan.md` at 5000 was a double) |
| F18 | Medium | In the AI sidebar's "Search chats" field, Delete with words after the caret archived the highlighted chat instead of deleting a character (Shift+Delete asked to delete the chat) (found by the re-audit) | Fixed | The field's own keydown took every Delete as a list action and prevented the default | Delete goes to the list only when there is nothing after the caret and no selection (`deletesTyped`) | `typed-delete.test.ts` (2 tests); ai-sidebar suite 113 passed; the key in the field not run in a browser |
| F19 | Medium | Choosing Dictate from the slash menu in a note only removed the typed `/dict`; dictation never started (regression from #2101; found by the re-audit) | Fixed | The slash rows are built once with no editor, and the record rows wrapped each row as `apply: () => row.run()`, dropping the editor the slash list passes, so the row ran with `editor = undefined` | A record row asked for another editor than the one it was built with is read again for that editor and run there | `commands/test/record-rows.test.ts` (failed before: the slash row got no editor); commands and dictation suites 203 passed |
| F20 | Medium | Picking a block not yet named after `[[#^` (a block of the same note, above the caret) wrote the link over the text before it and left the typed `[[#^` behind: `first block\n\nsee [[#^` became `first block ^abc123\n[[#^abc123]]see [[#^` (found by the re-audit) | Fixed | The completion waited for the block namer, which writes ` ^id` into the note, and then inserted at the `from`/`to` read before the wait | After the wait the typed range is found again: unmoved, or moved by what was written before it; if neither holds the link is not written | `wikilink/block-name.test.ts` (failed before with the output above); editor wikilink suite 112 passed, tsc clean |
| F21 | Medium | The quick add "Start date" (picked or typed, e.g. "Pay rent tomorrow") was saved as the todo's deadline and its start never set, so the todo showed at once with a deadline nobody set; with both a start and a deadline picked the start was dropped. Agents' and the glasses' `addTask` did the same (found by the re-audit) | Fixed | #2104 moved quick add onto todos and wrote `deadline: fields.deadline ?? fields.due`, while `fields.due` is what the Start date control sets and todos have a `start` of their own | `fields.due` is written as `start`, `fields.deadline` as `deadline`, in quick add and in `tasks-model` `addTask` | `quick-add/src/write.test.ts` (failed before); quick-add and tasks-model suites 61 passed; the `addTask` change is the same line, not tested on its own |
| F22 | Medium | A shortcut rebound, or a phone toolbar laid out, while signed in but unable to reach the account was undone at the next launch: the account's old keys came back over it (found by the re-audit) | Fixed | `share()` swallowed a failed save and kept no record of it, and `receive()` always took what the account held | A failed save leaves an owed mark (`orden:shortcuts-owed`, `orden:toolbar-owed`); while it is there `receive()` sends what this device holds instead of taking the account's, and a save that lands clears it | `shortcuts/src/owed.test.ts` (3 tests; the two relaunch tests failed before); shortcuts suite 288 passed |
| F23 | Medium | The same for every other account setting (Silent, quiet marks, vim, ligatures and the rest of `modes`): a change made while signed out or while the account could not be reached was owed only in memory, so after a restart the account's old value came back over it | Fixed | `modes` kept what it owed the account in a field that a restart emptied, and `adopt` then took the account's values | What is owed is kept in `orden:modes-owed`, so `adopt` sends it before it reads the account after a restart too; signing out still drops it | `modes.test.ts` "a change the account was not told of before a restart is sent first…" (failed before); modes suite 164 passed, golden snapshots included |
| F24 | Medium | A todo's space was the space's id on this device, which every other device and the account's MCP server do not know: "Only this space" on another device, or a todo Claude added through the account, never matched, the editor showed a raw id as the space, and `update_task` with `space: ""` put the todo in the open space instead of none | Fixed | The todos window and the desktop agent tools wrote `workspace.activeSpace.id`, a per-device `identifier()`, while todos sync through the account and the server keeps account space ids; `space: ""` was read as "not said" and fell back to the open space | A todo's space is the account id of a synced space (`todoSpaceOf`, through `sync.store`'s `remoteIdFor`), the local id when the space is not synced; filters, the editor and `list_tasks` also accept the older local id, and `space: ""` means no space | `tasks-views/test/account-space.test.ts` (2 failed before) and `agent-verbs.test.ts` "a todo in a synced space carries the account id…" (failed before); tasks-model, tasks-views and agent-verbs suites 130 passed |
| F25 | Medium | In a pane set to stack its tabs, clicking or tabbing into another column's text left the front column active, so Cmd/Ctrl+W, find, the format bar and the palette acted on a note other than the one being typed in | Fixed | Only a column's spine button called `workspace.activate`; the pane's pointerdown only focused the pane, and nothing followed focus moving into another column's editor | A column takes focus into account: `onfocusin` on each column makes it the pane's active tab (and the pane the focused one) unless it already is | `Pane.test.ts` "a column of a stacked pane…" (failed before); pane-tree suite 31 passed |
| F26 | Medium (test) | `chat.test.ts` "a chat started outside the panel > is drawn where it is watched…" failed about 1 run in 4 with "expected +0 to be 2", and under CPU load a dozen other tests late in the file timed out the same way | Fixed | Not a race in the chat store: every `launched()` resets the modules, so each test left one more live `Windows` behind on the real `BroadcastChannel`. Each new one said hello, every leaked one started watching it through Node's Web Locks, and the lock manager's work grew with every test until, late in the file, a `setTimeout(0)` waited about 0.9 s (CPU profile: `locks.request`/`query` under `Windows.watch`), past `vi.waitFor`'s 1 s once the machine was busy | The file gives every chat a `BroadcastChannel` that reaches no other window; its cross-window tests already drive `heard`/`heardWritten` directly | The stall after `ended` went from 885 ms to 1 ms and the file from 25 s to 12 s; 8 parallel runs failed 15 of 16 times before and passed 16 of 16 after |
| F27 | Medium | Since #2104 a few todo controls do nothing: Quick add's Reminder, Duration, time of day and Repeat are dropped when the todo is written, and Settings' "Reminders > Automatic" and "Show tasks without a start date in Today" are read by nothing | Left as is (David's choice) | A todo holds title, description, labels, space, start, deadline and priority only; `quick-add/src/write.ts` has nowhere to put the rest, and `modes.remindBefore`/`modes.undatedInToday` lost their readers with the note-shaped inbox | None: David chose to leave the controls as they are | Checked by reading `write.ts`, `tasks-model/src/todo.ts` and every reader of both settings |
| F28 | Medium | Regression of F22: the shortcuts and toolbar marks of what is owed to the account outlived sign-out, so after signing into another account this device's keys and toolbar overwrote that account's; and an older save landing after a newer one failed cleared the mark, so the newer change was undone at the next launch | Fixed | The marks sat in device storage with nothing tying them to the session, and every save, not only the latest, decided the mark | `owedUntilSignOut` drops each mark with the session (as `modes` does), and only the latest save of a key clears or sets it | `owed.test.ts` "a key owed to one account is not carried into the next…" and "an older save landing after a newer one failed…" (both failed before); shortcuts suite 290 passed |
| F29 | Medium | A theme, accent or light/dark picked while the account could not be reached was undone at the next launch: the account's old look came back over it | Fixed | `LookSync` kept what it owed the account in a field that a restart emptied (the same flaw F23 fixed in `modes`), so `adopt` read the account and wore its look | What is owed is kept in `orden:look-owed`, so `adopt` sends the pick first after a restart too; signing out still drops it | `look-sync.test.ts` "sends a pick that could not be sent before a restart…" (failed before); theme-looks suite 293 passed |
| F30 | Medium | On a board (or cards) over a database of tasks grouped by Status, Priority, a date, Tags, Assignee, Day or Overdue, a dragged card lit up the columns but snapped back and nothing was written | Fixed | #2104 took the task drops out of `views/src/drop.ts`, while `droppableBy` still offered those groupings and task rows are still shown and edited in databases | `dropInto` turns a drop on a task field into the same task change a cell edit makes again: done/status, priority, start/deadline through `startField`/`deadlineField` (Day moves an overdue deadline too), tags swapped, assignee | `views/src/drop.test.ts` (2 of 3 failed before); views suite 146 passed |
| F31 | Medium | In a database filtered by a tag (`file.hasTag("project")`) and grouped by tags, Add or New page in the "urgent" group wrote only `tags: urgent`, so the new note lost the base's tag and dropped out of the view the moment it was made | Fixed | `addNote` laid the group's value and the asked properties over `rowPlace`'s properties key by key, so a list the base needs was replaced instead of joined | `joinedProperties` joins a key holding a list in any layer into one list without repeats; plain keys still take the later value | `add.test.ts` "a note added in a tags group keeps the tag the base asks for…" (the old overwrite gave `tags: urgent`); views suite 147 passed |
| F32 | Medium | Numbered list (Cmd+Shift+[, the editor menu, the palette) put numbers before bullets and checkboxes (`- one` became `1. - one`) and flattened a nested list into one run of numbers; Bulleted list put a bullet before numbers (`1. one` became `- 1. one`) | Fixed | `toggleOrderedList` only knew `1.` markers and replaced from the start of the line, indentation included; `toggleBulletList` only knew `-*+` markers. A test pinned the stacked output under the name "converts a bullet list to an ordered one" | Both replace whatever list marker a line has after its indentation; numbering counts per indentation level, and a checkbox stays | `commands.test.ts` "converts a bullet list…" (now `1. one`), "keeps the checkboxes and the nesting…" and "turns a numbered list into bullets…" (all three failed before); editor suite 2076 passed |
| F33 | Medium (test) | `packages/editor` `blocks-nearby.test.ts` "only the blocks around the edit are looked at again" failed in the full suite with "expected … to have a length of 59 but got 30" | Fixed | Not a decorations bug: CodeMirror parses an edit for at most 20 ms (`Work.Apply`) inside the update, and on a busy machine it stops before the end; the block field then rightly rebuilds every block past where the parse stopped (`rebuiltAround`, `oldEnd !== newTree.length`), so the count of reused widgets depended on the machine's load | The test now checks that the blocks before the edit are always kept and asks for 59 only when the edit was parsed to the end; a new test makes `Date.now` run out the budget and checks the busy path keeps the blocks before the edit and draws the same as a fresh build | The forced-busy run gave exactly the reported "got 30" with the old assertion; blocks-nearby 8 passed |
| F34 | Medium | Rows copied or cut in the sidebar of one space and pasted into a folder of another space did nothing, with no notice, and a cut row stayed marked as cut | Fixed | `FileClipboard.paste` kept only paths `workspace.entryAt` finds, which looks in the open space's tree only, and `copyRows` and `move`'s kind need that entry too | The clipboard keeps the rows' entries from when they were copied (following renames and deletes), and `workspace.carrying` lets `entryAt` find them while that paste copies or moves them | `list-landing.test.ts` "rows copied or cut in one space are pasted into another" (failed before); workspace suite 990 passed |
| F35 | Medium | Paste from the editor's right-click menu while in a table cell put the text wherever the note's caret last was (often outside the table); Cut and Copy were greyed out for text selected in the cell | Fixed | A focused cell never moves the CodeMirror selection, and the menu's clipboard rows read and wrote `view.state.selection` only | The menu finds the editable cell it was opened on (`editableCellAt`); Paste hands the clipboard text to that cell (`pasteIntoCell`, the same path as Cmd+V in a cell), and Cut/Copy follow the cell's own selection | `table/menu-paste.test.ts` "text pasted from the menu over a table cell lands in that cell…"; table suite 106 passed. Bold and the other format rows of that menu still act on the note's selection (Low) |
| F36 | Medium | Since F21 a typed day is a todo's start, so "Pay rent by Friday", "Report due tomorrow" or "Miete zahlen bis Freitag" hid the todo until that day and gave it no deadline, from quick add and also from the glasses, shared text and agents, which show no chip | Fixed (David confirmed) (decision card) | The parser files every day under one `due` slot, whatever word led it; only `{…}` filled the deadline | A day led by `by`/`due` (`bis` in German) without a time fills the deadline (`deadlineWords` in the grammar); a plain "Friday" stays the start. Kept as its own commit so it can be dropped if David picks otherwise | `parse.test.ts` "a day said with by, due or bis is the deadline" (5 cases; the 4 rows that pinned the old reading moved there); bases suite 1052 and quick add, todos, glasses, phone and agent suites 1162 passed |
| F37 | Medium | Found by the bases thread: a card dragged across a column and a lane at once on a board of tasks (columns by status, lanes by priority) wrote nothing | Fixed | `inTurn` in `BoardLayout.svelte` merged only the `note` part of the two drop changes, so two task changes (F30) became `{ note: {} }` | `inTurn` moved to `views/src/drop.ts` and joins the note, task and list parts | `drop.test.ts` failed first (`inTurn` merged nothing), passes with the views tests (152) |
| F38 | Medium | Found by the bases thread: New page or Add in a column grouped by a link (project `[[Projects/X\|X]]`) wrote nothing, or wrote `Projects/X` as plain words, so the card landed in a new column | Fixed | `groupProperties` kept only words, numbers, truths and dates, and `addNote` wrote asked values through `plainValue`, which turns a link into its bare target | `add.ts` writes a link as `[[target\|display]]` for the group and for asked values, lists included | `add.test.ts` failed first (`{}` for a link group), passes with the views tests |
| F39 | Medium | Found by the bases thread: a note made from a template with `tags: [project]` in the "urgent" group of a tags board got `tags: [urgent]`, losing the template's tag | Fixed | F31's `joinedProperties` joined the base's, group's and asked lists, but `withProperties` then replaced the template's own list | `withTemplate` joins the template's lists into the properties the note is written with, for the keys the note sets | `add.test.ts` failed first (`withTemplate` missing, then tag lost), passes with the views tests |

### Frontend verification

- Full suite on Node 24, one package at a time, before F37: all green except the two known container failures (`@orden/pass` ssh-keygen missing, `@orden/machine` fork bomb under root). `packages/editor` `drag-start.test.ts` failed once and passed in 8 reruns since; handed to the editor core thread as a Low lead.
- After F37 to F39: clock sweep, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm check` (one package at a time), `pnpm format:check` and `pnpm knip` all green, after making `PASTE_TEXT` private.
- After merging `unstable` (#2101), at `2a093c00`: full suite one package at a time without `@orden/machine` (desktop 17,677 passed, editor 2,078, bases 1,052, sync 3,063, every other package green) with only the known `@orden/pass` ssh-keygen failure; `pnpm lint`, `pnpm check`, `pnpm format:check` and `pnpm knip` green.

### Frontend Lows left

Clear formatting strips stray characters; quote toggles stack; the menu's format rows act on the note's selection inside a table cell; `rowPlace` keeps quoted values raw; zoned datetimes lose their zone; equal todo order values; `bin()` hard-deletes a large held folder without undo; `dropSpace` matches the trash with `/` on Windows; overlapping undo batches; `connect.ts` treats a failed `readSecret` as no key; `shiftSince` tries a shift of 0 first.

## Bases, boards and to-dos

Burn-down of 2026-11-15 from `unstable` at `fb58ff0a`: bases (`packages/bases`), database views and
boards (`views`), to-dos (`tasks-model`, `tasks-views`) and quick add. Tests on Node 24. Findings the
frontend thread already fixed (F2, F4, F5, F12, F13, F21, F24, F30, F31, F36) and F27 (left as is)
are not repeated here.

| # | Severity | Bug | Status | Root cause | Fix | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BB1 | High | A todo ticked or changed on a device whose clock is behind the account (behind Claude's `add_task`/`update_task` through the account, or behind another device) was not changed: the tick sprang back and nothing was sent | Fixed | `TodoRecords.stamp` stamped an edit with the device's own clock; `mergedInbox` keeps a field only for a newer stamp, so an edit stamped below the field's stamp from the account was dropped on the spot | An edit is stamped at least one millisecond past the stamp each changed field holds (`justAfter`), so it always wins over what this device has seen | `tasks-model/test/stamps.test.ts` "keeps the change over one stamped later by the account" (failed before: `done: false, priority: 0`); tasks-model suite passed |
| BB2 | Medium | A todo with a long description or many subtasks (8000 accented characters, or 50 long subtasks: all within the limits) never reached the account or other devices, silently | Fixed | Every change to the account must fit `MOST_INBOX_BYTES` (16 KB) and a pulled item is dropped past it too, while `checkedPatch` counts characters only; `sending` skipped the change that did not fit and left it dirty for ever | `fitsOneChange` refuses an add or update whose whole todo would not fit one change, with a reason the editor shows | `stamps.test.ts` "is refused instead of staying on this device only…" (failed before); tasks-model suite passed |
| BB3 | Medium | The todo editor kept showing the title and description it opened with when they changed on another device, and a focus and blur of the field (no typing) wrote the old text back over the newer one | Fixed | The editor copied `title`/`description` into its fields only when another todo was opened, and saved on blur whenever the field differed from the todo | The fields follow the todo while they hold what they were last given; what is being typed is kept | `tasks-views/test/TodoEditor.effect.test.ts` (2 tests; the follow test failed before with `'Call Ann'`); tasks-views suite passed |
| BB4 | Medium | A task longer than 500 characters was refused: from Shortcuts/Siri it blocked every later Shortcuts task for good (the queue stops at the first that fails), from the phone's share sheet it was dropped, from quick add or the todos panel nothing happened (the panel also emptied its field) | Fixed | `addTask`, quick add's `write` and the panel's `addTodo` passed the whole text as the title, which `checkedPatch` refuses past `LIMITS.title` | `titledOf` cuts the title at the limit (with an ellipsis) and keeps the text as the description, ahead of any description typed (cut to what one change to the account holds, see BB19) | `tasks-model/test/long-text.test.ts` (3 tests, all failed before); tasks-model, tasks-views and quick-add suites passed |
| BB5 | Medium | A column, group, sort or summary over a property whose name reads like an expression (`due-date`, `e-mail`, `Size (cm)`, a key `a.b`) showed nothing: every cell empty, every row in one "No value" group | Fixed | `getter` read a property id through `compile`, which turns `note.due-date` into `note.due - date` and `note.Size (cm)` into a call; it fell back to the front matter key only when compiling failed | When the note holds a key of that very name (after `note.`, or bare), it is read; otherwise the expression runs as before (`keyNamed`) | `answer-keys.test.ts` "is read from the front matter key…" and "groups by its values" (both failed before), and "still reads a nested value or an expression…"; bases suite passed |
| BB6 | Medium | A view filtered by `this` (`status == this.status`, an embedded base or a fence) kept showing the old rows after the note it sits in changed | Fixed | The per-row facts were cached under the host's path and space only, not what it holds, and rows that did not change keep their objects | The cache key holds an id per host row object (`thisId`); the rows store gives a changed note a new row | `answer-keys.test.ts` "answers again once the note it sits in changes" (failed before: `a.md`); answer and answer-cache suites passed |
| BB7 | Medium | Writing a base (renaming a view, any change) dropped keys it does not know on a select option (`colour`, `icon`) and on `orden.id` (`start`), so an older Orden on another device wipes what a newer one wrote there | Fixed | `readOptions` and the id were rebuilt from the known fields only, without the `kept` every other part of the file has | Options and the id keep what they do not know and write it back; the board's colour menu and the options editor change a copy of the option instead of building a new one from value, group and tone | `base-file.test.ts` "stay on an option and on the id…" (failed before); base-file suite passed; the two menu paths are the same one-line change, not tested on their own |
| BB8 | Medium | The Todoist filter import refused `Assigned to: me`, `Due before: tomorrow`, `Created after: -7 days` ("is not a filter") though it reads them in lower case | Fixed | The keyed-term pattern ran on the typed text without the `i` flag | The pattern takes either case; the key is lower-cased after as before | `todoist.test.ts` "reads as it does in lower case" (failed before); todoist suite passed |
| BB9 | Low | Ticking a 🏁 delete task in a note with CRLF line breaks left an empty line; the next occurrence of a repeating task was written with a bare LF | Fixed | The deleted range added one character for the line break, which in CRLF is the `\r`; the new line was joined with `\n` | The range runs to where the next line starts; the next occurrence takes the note's line break | `occurrence.test.ts` (2 tests, both failed before); occurrence suite passed |
| BB10 | Medium | Quick add read a time said before the part of the day wrongly and left words in the title: "call mom at 7 tomorrow morning" gave 19:00 and "call mom morning", "at 8 tonight" gave 19:00, "Anruf um 8 heute Abend" gave 08:00; "call friday morning" set no time and kept "morning"; "Pay every 15th of the month" and "Pay every day at 9 starting tomorrow" left "of the month" and "starting" in the title | Fixed | `whenAt` refined a time only by a part of the day said before it, and after a weekday read a part of the day only after on/am; `repeatAt` read starting/until only right after the rule and nothing read "of the month"/"des Monats" | A time is shifted into a day-and-part or part word that follows it (`inPart`, shared with the part-then-time order); a part of the day after a day is its time (`partOfDayAt`); `ofTheMonth` words are read after a monthly rule, and one time may stand before starting/until | `parse.test.ts` "on a Sunday in November" (16 cases, all failed before); bases suite 1074 passed |
| BB11 | High | Editing a task's date, duration, status or priority in a table cell corrupted the task line: `📅 [object Object]`, `[duration:: NaNhNaNm]`, `- [Done] buy milk` (no longer a task, gone from every view), `undefined` for a priority; typing `x` skipped the tick path, so a repeating task got no next occurrence | Fixed | #2104 removed the task cell editors while `TASK_EDITORS` still named them, so every task field fell through to `PropertyValue` and `changeOf` copied its `Value` into `TaskChange` as it was | `changeOf` builds each task field through `taskChange`: a day for dates, minutes for duration, 1 to 6 for priority (number, `p2` or the words), done/undone through the same change a tick makes, other status characters the parser takes; words that are none of these write nothing. The duration field is filled with `writeDuration` | `cell-writes.effect.test.ts` (21 cases, 17 failed before with the symptoms above); views and rows suites passed |
| BB12 | High | Delete on a task card sent the whole note that holds the task to the bin without asking (with its other tasks and body); Duplicate copied the whole note and Copy link linked the note | Fixed | #2104 removed the task branches of the card menu along with `lines.ts` | `deleteTask`, `duplicateTask` and `linkToTask` act on the task's block (the line and what is indented under it) through `task-block.ts`, as one undoable change; a note card is unchanged | `task-card-menu.test.ts` (3 tests, all failed before) |
| BB13 | Medium | Add, New page and the form in a database written in a note (a fence, e.g. "New database") made the note in the open space's root instead of beside the database's note, so it failed the database's own `file.folder == this.file.folder` filter and vanished; in a split pane on another space it went to the open space | Fixed | A fence's kit has no `.base` file, and `addNote` fell back to the active space's root | The kit's `home` is the `.base` file or else the note the fence sits in; adding and the "can add here" check use it | `fence-home.test.ts` "adds its notes beside the note it sits in…" (failed before: `null`) |
| BB14 | Medium | The filter builder rewrote a compound filter that starts with `today()`/`now()` (`task.date >= today() && task.date < today() + "7d"`, what its own Todoist field writes) as one quoted value on the next change, emptying the view | Fixed | `unliteral` took anything starting with `today()` as a value, unlike `literal`, which is anchored | Both use one anchored `CLOCK` pattern | `filter-rows-clock.test.ts` (2 tests; the compound one failed before) |
| BB15 | Medium | A checkbox column showed no checkbox on a note without that key, so it could not be ticked | Fixed | The checkbox was drawn only for a boolean value | It is drawn unticked for no value too | `cell-writes.effect.test.ts` |
| BB16 | Medium | A number cell opened empty from 1000 up ("1,500" is not a number to the field), every decimal opened empty in a comma-decimal region, and 3.14159 opened as 3.142 | Fixed | The field got the localized display text | It gets the number as it is | `cell-writes.effect.test.ts` |
| BB17 | Medium | Picking 3 in a select over numeric options (`[1, 2, 3]`, `priority: 1`) wrote `priority: '3'`, which since B296 reads as text in filters, sorts and sums | Fixed | Option values are kept as strings and the cell wrote the picked words | `optionWritten` writes a number when the words are a plain number and the property holds numbers (the row's own value first, then the column, then all options numeric); `"007"`-style text stays text | `cell-writes.effect.test.ts` (numeric and text-stays-text cases) |
| BB18 | Medium | Since #2104, + and Q on a database of tasks made an empty `Untitled.md` that view never shows, and a button's "Add task" did nothing | Fixed, pending David's answer on the decision card (kept as its own commit so it can be dropped) | #2104 took task adding out of views; `kit.add` always adds a note and `pressButton` no longer reads `task` | A database of tasks does not offer adding (`kit.adds`), and the button builder no longer offers Add task | `fence-home.test.ts` "of tasks offers no adding…" |
| BB19 | Medium | A task from long text in a script of several bytes a character (Chinese, emoji, 6000 characters) was still refused, and a title cut could split an emoji in two | Fixed | BB4 kept the whole text as the description, but one change to the account is measured in bytes (16 KB), and the title was cut by UTF-16 units | The description is cut to 12000 bytes as the account measures it (`cutToBytes`), the title by whole characters (`wholeSlice`) | `long-text.test.ts` "a script of several bytes…" (failed before); tasks-model suite passed |
| BB20 | Medium | Quick add "every monday morning" or "jeden Montag abends" set no time and left the part of the day in the title | Fixed | `repeatAt` read a time after the rule but no part of the day | A part of the day after the rule is its time (`partSaid`, `timeInPart`) | `parse.test.ts` repeating-day parts (failed before); bases suite passed |
| BB21 | Low | After BB10, "every day bis morgen" no longer read "bis morgen" as the repeat's end | Fixed | BB10 let a time stand before starting/until and tried that first, so "bis" was taken for a time word | `boundAt` tries starting/until right after the rule first, then after a time | `parse.test.ts` "bis morgen" (failed before) |
| BB22 | Low | Duplicate on a task card kept the task's 🆔 id, so the copy was a second task of that id (dependencies point at both) | Fixed | `copiedLine` dropped the block id only | It drops the 🆔 id too, through `changedTask`, keeping the CRLF | `task-card-menu.test.ts` "Duplicate leaves out the task id…" (failed before) |
| BB23 | Low | After a title saved with spaces trimmed, the todo editor stopped following changes from elsewhere | Fixed | The field kept the typed text, which no longer matched what it was given, so it counted as typing | The field takes the saved title | `TodoEditor.effect.test.ts` third test (failed before) |
| BB24 | Low | Changing a numbered base's id prefix in the options builder dropped the id's other keys (BB7's `kept`) | Fixed | The builder built a new id from property and prefix | It changes a copy of the id | Same one-line pattern as BB7, read through |
| BB25 | Low | After one edit over a stamp from a clock far ahead (a device set to next year), every later edit on this device was stamped past it too | Fixed | `stamp` raised its own clock (`last`) to the floor | The floor is used for that stamp only | `stamps.test.ts` "far ahead" (failed before) |
| BB26 | Medium | Moving a timeline bar by days wrote `2026-01-07T10` for a time on the hour, which reads as no time | Fixed | `timeIn` cut a trailing `:00` from `10:00`, meaning seconds but taking the minutes | Only `:00` seconds after hours and minutes are cut | `timeline-time.test.ts` (failed before: `2026-01-07T10`) |
| BB27 | High | Saving a note while its space was still being read at start (an open note autosaving, sync bringing a change) ran the base's "added" automations on it: `status: Inbox` over `Done`, and a numbered base gave it a new id over its own | Fixed | During the first read the store's rows are empty, so the change it told said the note had no rows before, which automations take for a note just made | A change told before the space's first read is done says so (`firstRead`), and automations leave it; re-reads keep the rows they had, so they are not marked | `store.test.ts` "a note saved before its space is first read…" and `running.test.ts` "a note saved before its space is first read" (both failed before) |
| BB28 | Medium | Two notes reaching a numbered base within moments of each other (a move of several, a template run, sync) were given the same id | Fixed | Each note's automation ran on its own timer and read the rows before the other's write was in | Runs in a space wait for the one before; a write is in the rows by the time it ends | `running.test.ts` "notes that reach a numbered base at about the same time" (failed before: two runs at once) |
| BB29 | Medium | Dragging a card to a column of a board grouped by numeric options (`[1, 2, 3]`), or adding one there, wrote `priority: '3'`, which reads as text in filters, sorts and sums (BB17 on another path) | Fixed | A column made from an option is keyed by the option's words, and the drop and the add wrote that key as it was | Drops (`kit.drop`, the board's own drag, lanes too) and adds (`kit.add`, the column's new card) write the key through `keyWritten`, BB17's `optionWritten` for a group key | `board-option-keys.test.ts` (3 tests; drop and add failed before with `'3'`) |
| BB30 | Medium | Moving a timeline bar by days on a date written in UTC (`2026-01-07T10:00:00Z`) wrote `2026-01-09T10:00UTC`, which reads as text, so filters, sorts and date summaries lost the note | Fixed | A `Z` is read as the zone `UTC`, and `timeIn` wrote the zone back as it was held | `timeIn` writes `UTC` as `Z`; numeric offsets were already kept | `timeline-time.test.ts` "keeps a time written in UTC with its Z" (failed before) |
| BB31 | Low | A long task's description could end in half an emoji when the cut at 8000 characters fell inside one; quick add with long text and a long typed description was refused | Fixed | The character cut ran before the byte cut and only the byte cut kept whole characters; quick add joined the two after the cut | `fittedDescription` cuts by whole characters, then to bytes, and quick add runs the joined text through it | `long-text.test.ts` (2 tests, both failed before) |
| BB32 | Low | Right after a save in the app, `file.size` of a note with accents or emoji was its length in UTF-16 units, while the scan of the disk gives bytes, so size filters and sorts shifted (found by the search thread, their N16) | Fixed | The rows store stamped saved and read text with `content.length` | It stamps the UTF-8 byte length | `store.test.ts` "a note saved or read here gives its size in bytes" (failed before: 9 for 13) |

Left as Low after the re-audit: "at 12 tonight" reads as noon; ticking a todo that was already too big for one change is refused with the reason, as any edit to it; a database embedded with `![[x.base]]` adds where the `.base` file is, not beside the note; a part of the day said alone ("tonight") and with a time use different default hours. A note made while its space is still being read at start gets no "added" automation (BB27 leaves it rather than risk overwriting one that existed). From the final diff review: a `task.done` column in a view of notes and tasks draws an unticked box on a note row that ticks nothing; deleting or duplicating the last line of a CRLF note with no final line break leaves a `\r` or adds a bare LF; dropping into a board date group writes the day without its zone.

Found by the views audit in code the frontend thread had changed, and fixed there on `claude/project-thread-jsjtvs` (396a430b, 961c4602): a diagonal board drag lost its task part (`inTurn`), New page in a link group wrote plain text, and a template's lists were replaced instead of joined.

## Settings and onboarding

Burn-down from 2026-11-15 on `claude/project-thread-1r6eak`, from `unstable` at `fb58ff0a`.
Area: settings screens and persistence, the first-launch setup (#2085), the "Show me around"
guide, themes, keyboard shortcuts and the strings in them. Rows are numbered `SO` so they do not
collide with the other areas' rows. Device-only request counts, "how did you hear" answers and
cross-device resume need server work and are not bugs.

| # | Severity | Bug | Status | Root cause | Fix | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SO1 | Medium | In setup's "Connect your AI", every refused key for OpenRouter, Mistral, DeepSeek or Grok added another provider, and a refused Claude/ChatGPT/Gemini key left a new account with a bad key, all shown later under Assistants | Fixed `3d9dd473` | `connectCompatible` and `connectKey` add the account before `keyed` writes the key and lists models; on a failure only the keyless path removed it | `keyed` forgets the key and removes the account when this attempt made it; an account that was there before stays | `connect.test.ts` (3 tests, 2 failed before); onboarding 86 passed |
| SO2 | Medium | After signing in during setup, Back on Appearance went to "Log in or sign up" again, with the email field, while signed in; a sign-in or browser import finishing after Back moved the setup on from the wrong step | Fixed `30adb44c` | The trail kept steps that no longer show (`account` once signed in), `setup.signedIn` was never set after a sign-in in setup, and the delayed and awaited `next()` calls moved on from whatever step was showing | Back skips steps that no longer show; the account step sets `signedIn`; `next(from)` moves on only from the step that asked | `setup.test.ts` "never goes back to the log in once signed in", "moves on late only from the step that asked for it" (failed before); onboarding 86 passed |
| SO3 | Medium | Recording Cmd/Ctrl+Backspace or Delete for a shortcut (the default of "Delete the selected files") cleared the shortcut instead | Fixed `1a413553` | `Rebind.record` took any Backspace or Delete as "clear", whatever was held | Clear only when no Ctrl, Cmd or Alt is held; otherwise it is read as a combination | `rebind.test.ts` "takes Backspace or Delete held with Ctrl or Alt as the key" (failed before); settings and shortcuts 414 passed |
| SO4 | Medium | A canvas tool (Rectangle `r`, ...), Group/Ungroup, quick add `q` and Space to look could not be given a single key again, not even their own default, once changed | Fixed `b01d1427` | `refuse` turned down every bare single key; the list and own-key exceptions only covered typing keys | The exceptions cover single keys too | `shortcuts.test.ts` "and a list's own row may have a letter, where nobody is typing" (failed before); 414 passed |
| SO5 | Medium | Spelling "Reset to defaults" on Linux left every dictionary that comes before the reader's own one in the list on (e.g. Deutsch and English instead of English) | Fixed `ec26827e` | `resetFields` walked the fields in order, and turning off the only dictionary on is refused, so it stayed | Fields that turn on are reset before the ones that turn off | `spelling-pane.test.ts` "go back to the reader's own language alone on a reset" (failed before); settings and glasses-choices 186 passed |
| SO6 | High | A shortcut or toolbar change made while the account could not hear it (offline, or during the launch's read) was undone by the account's older copy on the next launch, locally and then on the server | Fixed `fe609bc6` | `receive` always let the account win; a failed save was dropped and nothing remembered it was owed | `AccountOwed` keeps, per account, that a change has not been heard yet; while it stands `receive` sends ours instead of taking theirs, and a heard save clears it | `account-owed.test.ts` (4 tests, 3 failed before): survives a relaunch, survives a read in flight, not pressed on another account, toolbar too; 419 passed |
| SO7 | Medium | Every other synced setting (ligatures, Vim, attachments, conflicts, markdown display, ...) changed offline was lost the same way after a restart | Fixed `c7d68d00` | `modes`' owed patch lived only in memory and was cleared before the save was heard | The owed patch is kept on disk with the account it belongs to, cleared field by field once heard; `adopt` takes nothing from the account while some is owed | `modes.test.ts` "keeps a change the account never heard across a restart, and sends it before reading" (failed before); modes 164 passed |
| SO8 | Medium | An agent writing a shortcut through the settings tools could give it a key another command already runs, with no warning; Settings then showed two commands on one key | Fixed `46874dcf` | The declared field's `check` ran `refuse` only, never `conflicts` | `check` also names the command that already runs the key | `schema.effect.test.ts` "is turned down where another command already runs that key" (failed before); settings 165 passed |
| SO9 | Medium | Leaving the Wallpaper theme (even hovering another theme in the picker) and coming back left only the heavily blurred copy until restart | Fixed `cd68ad48` | Leaving removes `--wallpaper-sharp` but did not reset the scheme tracker, so coming back in the same scheme never wore it again | A `slept` flag makes the return wear the sharp copy whatever the scheme | `wallpaper.effect.test.ts` (failed before); wallpaper 122 passed |
| SO10 | Medium | For signed-in readers, a theme file whose name has a space or is long lost its own settings on every launch, and the server refused the whole look | Fixed `d0b7e156`, widened by the next commit | Values drawers were checked against the setting-name pattern, not the theme-id pattern, in both `valuesIn` and the server's `lookWrong` | A drawer is any name either pattern accepts | `look.test.ts` "keeps the values of a file theme under its own name" (failed before), `file:a/b` still refused; look-sync 10, sync server settings 80 passed |
| SO11 | Low | Update in the theme store switched the app to the updated theme | Fixed `08140aa3` | `install` always ended with `use` | Only for a theme not installed before or already in use | `store.test.ts` (failed before); gallery 109 passed |
| SO12 | High | Second look at SO6: a shortcut or toolbar change made before the account was known at launch was still undone, and after signing out and in as another account, the next sign-in of the first one sent the second account's shortcuts to it | Fixed `7389adc8` | `AccountOwed` wrote no marker without a known account and was never cleared on sign-out | The marker is written whatever is known, keeps the account it was owed to while none is known, and is forgotten on sign-out | `account-owed.test.ts` "is not undone once it is", "leaves nothing owed for the next account, nor for the one that left" (failed before); shortcuts 293 passed |
| SO13 | Medium | A setting owed to an account (say Vim) was dropped when another setting changed in a launch that did not know the account yet | Fixed `7389adc8` | The owed patch's owner was read as foreign while the account was unknown, so the next write replaced it | An owed patch counts as ours while no other account is known, and keeps its owner on write | `modes.test.ts` "keeps what is owed to an account through a launch that does not know the account yet" (failed before); modes 165 passed |
| SO14 | Medium | Sync's "Go back" rolled back to the preview's moment after Keep versions shrank (180 days while the picker said 30), or rolled the space now open back by another space's preview | Fixed `fa96918b` | The preview was kept with its moment only and used by Go back whatever the picker and space said | The preview keeps its days and space; it shows, and Go back runs, only while both still match | `SyncPane.effect.test.ts` "never goes back further than the moment the picker shows" (failed before); settings 131 passed |
| SO15 | Medium | A mistyped email in setup left the code screen with no way back to the email field | Fixed `8bb91ba6` | The account step passed no `onback`, and Back shows only with something earlier on the trail | While asking for a code, Back returns to the email, as the Sign in dialog's close does | `AccountStep.effect.test.ts` "goes back from the code to the email" (failed before); onboarding 88 passed |
| SO16 | Low | A second Enter during the screen's slide-out skipped the next question | Fixed `22c40585` | The outgoing screen keeps its window key handler until its outro ends, and its `next()` moved on from the new step | `next(from)` is required; every screen names its own step | `AccountStep.effect.test.ts` "moves nothing on a second Enter" (failed before) |
| SO17 | Low | A theme's switch for a theme not worn read off when nothing was kept, though its default is on; an agent writing off was then a silent no-op | Fixed `2ffaa492` | `get` compared the kept value with `true` and ignored the default | `get` falls back to the setting's default | `schema.effect.test.ts` "reads as its default until something is kept for it" (failed before) |
| SO18 | Medium | Regression from SO7 found by the final re-audit: one change the server refuses (a glasses command word over 60 characters, settings over 8 KB) stayed owed for good, so that device never sent or took another synced setting until sign-out | Fixed `dd601bc7` | `settle` and `AccountOwed` kept the owed state on any error, a refusal as much as being offline, and an owed state stops `adopt` taking the account's copy | A 4xx refusal (other than 401, 408, 429) counts as heard and drops what was owed; offline and 5xx keep it | `modes.test.ts` "lets go of a change the account turned down", `account-owed.test.ts` "is owed no longer, so the account is heard again" (both failed before); shortcuts, modes, accounts 569 passed |
| SO19 | Medium | A theme picked while offline, or before the account was known at launch, was undone by the account's older look on reconnecting; a pick that failed to send was lost on relaunch | Fixed `c0dde960` | `LookSync` marked a failed save owed only in memory, and a pick without a usable token was neither sent nor marked | `OwedMark` keeps, on disk and per account, that a pick is owed while signed in (a pick while signed out still stays on the device); `adopt` sends it before reading; a heard or refused save and sign-out clear it | `look-sync.test.ts` "outlives a relaunch and wins over the account's older one", "picked before the account is known is not undone once it is" (failed before); gallery 111 passed |
| SO20 | Medium | "Use as wallpaper" from another theme made the picture at the default blur and tone, whatever the Wallpaper theme's sliders said, until a slider moved or a relaunch | Fixed `8bdf667c` | `blurDial` and `toneDial` read `theme.values`, which belong to the theme worn, before the switch to Wallpaper | The dials read the Wallpaper theme's own kept values while it is not worn | `wallpaper.effect.test.ts` "is made at the wallpaper's own blur, not the default" (failed before); theme, theme-looks and effects 1323 passed |
| SO21 | Medium | Regression from SO12/SO7 found by re-audit round 2: a shortcut, toolbar or synced setting changed while signed out was owed for good, so the next sign-in replaced the account's customised set (shortcuts and toolbar wholly, other settings field by field) | Fixed `5bdd268e` | The owed marker was written whatever the session, and "signed out" could not be told from "the session is still being read at launch" | `account.noAccountForSure` (the session was looked for and there is none); nothing is owed then, as a theme pick already worked. While the session is still being read, changes are owed as before | `account-owed.test.ts` "stays on this device, and the account's own are taken at the next sign-in", `modes.test.ts` "keeps a change made while signed out on this device" (both failed before); 814 passed in modes, shortcuts, settings, gallery, accounts |
| SO22 | Medium | Re-audit round 3: the guest version of SO21. Shortcuts, settings or a theme changed in a guest session (from a share link) replaced the account's on signing in from the guest corner or Settings | Fixed `7b79ca08` | A guest has a token, so `noAccountForSure` was false and its changes were owed with no owner, which any account takes as its own | `noAccountForSure` is also true for a guest session with no account | `account.test.ts` "holds no account for sure, so nothing it changes is owed to the one it signs in to" (failed before); 815 passed |
| SO23 | Medium | Re-audit round 4: a shortcut, toolbar, setting or theme changed while the session was still being read at launch (a guest launched offline, or no session saved) stayed owed with no owner, so the next sign-in sent it over that account's own | Fixed `6f6b7dea` | A marker with no owner counts for any account, and nothing dropped it once the session turned out to hold none | `account.holdsNoAccount` tells the owed stores when restore finds no saved session or the account answers without a user; markers with no owner are dropped then | `account.test.ts` (2), `account-owed.test.ts` "is not sent over the account signed in to later" (failed before); 1915 passed in accounts, shortcuts, modes, settings, theme-looks, onboarding, glasses-choices and the touched mocks; svelte-check 0 errors |

| SO25 | Medium | Re-audit round 5: a shortcut written as `Ctrl+Shift+K` (how the app shows keys on Windows and Linux) or any word was taken through the agent settings tool, and the shortcut stopped working | Fixed `7f2bc3f8` | `parseCombination` splits on `-` only, so the whole text became a key name `refuse` never checked | `refuse` turns down a key that is neither one character, a named key (`F5`, `ArrowUp`, `ContextMenu`) nor a double tap | `shortcuts.test.ts` "is refused where it names no key the keyboard has" (failed before) |
| SO26 | Medium | Re-audit round 5: two canvas tools (or Explorer, Pages, table rows) could be given one key with no clash shown, and the later one could no longer be reached | Fixed `7f2bc3f8` | `conflicts` left every contextual row out, which is how separate places share the arrows, so clashes inside one place were missed too | A contextual row is checked against the rows of its own place (the id's first part) and nothing else; no shipped key clashes on any system | `shortcuts.test.ts` "counts the contextual ones that answer in the same place" (failed before), "finds no contextual row clashing as the keys ship, on any system" |
| SO27 | Medium | Re-audit round 5: Reset on a shortcut whose key another row had taken over brought the key back to both, with no clash shown, and the app one silently won | Fixed `34a520bf` | The row's Reset called `shortcuts.reset` directly, past the clash check every recorded key goes through | Reset goes through `Rebind.reset`, which shows the same "already runs ... Take it over" prompt; taking it over brings the default back as no override | `rebind.test.ts` "a key brought back by Reset" (failed before); shortcuts, settings, canvas, accounts, modes, onboarding and ui-kit 1696 passed; svelte-check 0 errors |
| SO28 | Medium | Re-audit round 6, regression from SO24: a change made during an offline launch was dropped when the same account signed in again by code (a new token), so the account's older values came back | Fixed `73deaa6b` | SO24 dropped every ownerless mark on any token change over an unanswered session, unable to tell the same account from another | Before dropping, the old token is asked who it holds (3 s at most); the marks stay when it names the account signing in, and go on any other answer, a refusal or no answer | `account.test.ts` "does not tell it when a code signs the same account in again" (failed before); the SO24 tests still pass; accounts, sharing, onboarding, modes, shortcuts and gallery 992 passed |
| SO29 | Medium | Re-audit round 6: explorer shortcuts gave no clash with the list keys the explorer also answers to (select all, delete, rename and the rest), so e.g. Show hidden set to Mod-A never fired | Fixed `fdde5671` | SO26's places compared ids by prefix only, and the explorer handles ten `tree.*` rows next to its own | Those ten `tree.*` rows count in the explorer's place as well as their own; no shipped key clashes on any system. A key of another script (Arabic Lam-Alef, astral characters) is no longer refused as no key | `shortcuts.test.ts` "counts the list keys the explorer answers to as its own place" (failed before), the shipped-keys sweep, "names no key" with `Mod-لا` |
| SO30 | Medium | Re-audit round 6: Start timer, Chats, Time, Pass and other buttons on the format bar were gone after a relaunch, and the next bar edit lost them for good; a bar edit on the web also took desktop-only buttons off the desktop bar | Fixed `40966ed6` | The stored and received list was filtered by what can be pressed right now, and at launch the toolbar is read before hidden kinds, so those commands counted as not there | The bar keeps every known command and only shows the ones this device can press; adding, removing and moving keep the hidden ones where they were | `toolbar.test.ts` "a button this device cannot press yet" (2, failed before); shortcuts, settings and surfaces 443 passed |
| SO31 | Medium | Re-audit round 6: a store theme in use that was removed (Remove in the Theme Store, or deleted and Reload themes) came back at the next relaunch or reconnect for a signed-in reader | Fixed `d7cc81cc` | `reload()` fell back to Classic through `wearId`, which never tells the look sync, so the account still named the theme with its css and wrote it back | After launch, the fallback picks Classic as a choice, so the account hears it; at launch it stays quiet so an account theme missing on this device is still brought back | `store.test.ts` "removing the theme in use tells the account" (failed before); theme, theme-looks, settings, commands 658 passed; svelte-check 0 errors |
| SO32 | Medium | Re-audit round 7, regression from SO28: when the old session answered while another account signed in (a share link shortly after launch), it signed in for a moment and the new account then took over without the old one's session being forgotten | Fixed `e74a7e55` | SO28 read who was signed in before awaiting the old token's answer, and nothing looked again after | Who was signed in is read after the wait, so an account that answered meanwhile is forgotten as on any switch | `account.test.ts` "forgets the old account when it answers while another one signs in" (failed before); accounts 117 passed |
